```js
  const cue = world.balls[0];
  if (game.ballInHand) {
    if (!Array.isArray(m.cue) || !num(m.cue[0]) || !num(m.cue[1]) || !C.validSpot(world.balls, m.cue[0], m.cue[1], 0, game.kitchen)) {
      toast(`Ignored a cue ball placement from ${NET.peerName} that breaks the rules`, 'foul'); return;
    }
    cue.x = m.cue[0]; cue.z = m.cue[1];
  }
  // the input-only check: recompute the strike here and only accept the sender's numbers if they agree
  const t = { ...cue }; C.strike(t, shot);
  const mine = [t.vx, t.vz, t.wx, t.wy, t.wz];
  let v = m.v;
  const ok = Array.isArray(v) && v.length === 5 && v.every((x, i) => num(x) && Math.abs(x - mine[i]) <= 1e-6 * Math.max(1, Math.abs(mine[i])));
  if (!ok) { v = mine; toast(`That shot's data didn't add up, so your game worked it out itself`, 'foul'); }
  Object.assign(aim, shot); NET.aimT = null; remoteStrike = v;
  beginStroke();
}
function netTick(now, dt) {
  if (!NET.on || !NET.started) return;
  if (state === 'aim' && humanTurn() && now - NET.lastAim > 80) {
    const cue = world.balls[0];
    const sig = `${aim.phi.toFixed(5)}|${aim.power.toFixed(4)}|${aim.sx.toFixed(3)}|${aim.sy.toFixed(3)}|${game.ballInHand ? cue.x.toFixed(4) + ',' + cue.z.toFixed(4) : ''}`;
    if (sig !== NET.aimSig) {
      NET.aimSig = sig; NET.lastAim = now;
      netSend({ t: 'aim', phi: aim.phi, power: aim.power, sx: aim.sx, sy: aim.sy, cue: game.ballInHand ? [cue.x, cue.z] : null });
    }
  }
  if (state === 'remote' && NET.aimT) {
    const t = NET.aimT, k = 1 - Math.exp(-dt * 14), n = v => typeof v === 'number' && isFinite(v);
    if (n(t.phi)) aim.phi = wrapA(aim.phi + wrapA(t.phi - aim.phi) * k);
    if (n(t.power)) aim.power = lerp(aim.power, clamp(t.power, 0, 1), k);
    if (n(t.sx) && n(t.sy)) { aim.sx = lerp(aim.sx, clamp(t.sx, -1, 1), k); aim.sy = lerp(aim.sy, clamp(t.sy, -1, 1), k); }
    if (game.ballInHand && Array.isArray(t.cue) && n(t.cue[0]) && n(t.cue[1])) {
      const cue = world.balls[0]; cue.x = lerp(cue.x, clamp(t.cue[0], -T.hl, T.hl), k); cue.z = lerp(cue.z, clamp(t.cue[1], -T.hw, T.hw), k);
    }
    updateShotPanel();
  }
}
function updateNetBadge() {
  const el = $('#net');
  if (!NET.on) { el.textContent = ''; return; }
  const ok = NET.link === 'online' && NET.peer;
  el.textContent = NET.link !== 'online' ? 'Reconnecting to the relay…' : ok ? `${NET.peerName} is connected` : `${NET.peerName} is not connected`;
  el.dataset.ok = ok ? '1' : '0';
}
// quick chat: preset messages only, so there's nothing to moderate in listed rooms
const CHAT = ['Nice shot!', 'Unlucky', 'Good luck', 'Good game', 'Thanks!', 'Oops!', 'Wow!', 'One more?'];
let lastChat = 0;
function sendChat(i) {
  if (!NET.on || performance.now() - lastChat < 1200) return;
  lastChat = performance.now(); netSend({ t: 'chat', i }); toast(`You: ${CHAT[i]}`, 'chat'); $('#chatPop').hidden = true;
}
CHAT.forEach((txt, i) => { const b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'btn'; b.textContent = txt; b.addEventListener('click', () => sendChat(i)); $('#chatPop').appendChild(b); });
$('#bChat').addEventListener('click', () => { $('#chatPop').hidden = !$('#chatPop').hidden; });
function concedeFrame(fromRemote, loser) {
  if (game.over || M.mode === 'practice') return false;
  if (state === 'moving' || state === 'stroke' || state === 'set' || state === 'botAim') { if (!fromRemote) toast('Wait for the balls to stop first', 'info'); return false; }
  if (loser !== 0 && loser !== 1) loser = NET.on ? NET.seat : (M.opp === 'bot' ? 0 : game.turn);
  const w = 1 - loser;
  game = { ...game, over: true, winner: w }; matchWins[w]++;
  state = 'over'; bot = null; $('#thinking').hidden = true; $('#offer').hidden = true; updateHUD();
  frameEarn(); if (CAR.on) careerFrameOver();
  showOver({ reason2: 'Conceded the frame' }, loser);
  return true;
}
$('#bConcede').addEventListener('click', () => { togglePause(false); if (concedeFrame(false) && NET.on) netSend({ t: 'concede' }); });
$('#bOfferRerack').addEventListener('click', () => {
  togglePause(false);
  if (!NET.on || game.over) return;
  netSend({ t: 'rerackOffer' }); toast(`Re-rack offered. Waiting for ${NET.peerName}`, 'info');
});
$('#bOfferYes').addEventListener('click', () => { $('#offer').hidden = true; netSend({ t: 'rerackYes' }); if (NET.seat === 0) hostStart(); });
$('#bOfferNo').addEventListener('click', () => { $('#offer').hidden = true; netSend({ t: 'rerackNo' }); });
function updateAgainBtn() {
  const b = $('#bAgain');
  const base = matchDone() ? 'New match' : raceTo() ? 'Next frame' : 'Play again';
  b.textContent = NET.again[NET.seat] ? `Waiting for ${NET.peerName}…` : NET.again[1 - NET.seat] ? `${base}: accept` : base;
}
function tryRematch() {
  if (!NET.on) return;
  if (NET.again[0] && NET.again[1]) { NET.again = [false, false]; if (NET.seat === 0) { NET.games++; hostStart(); } }
  else if (!$('#over').hidden) updateAgainBtn();
}
$('#bJoin').addEventListener('click', () => { ensureAudio(); sfx('ui'); startOnline(cleanCode($('#netCode').value)); });
// open rooms list: watched live from the relay while the online menu is showing
const LW = { ws: null, rooms: [], off: 0, retry: 0, timer: 0, want: false, failed: false, loaded: false };
const MODE_NAME = { '8ball': '8-ball', '9ball': '9-ball', uk8: 'Reds & yellows' };
function updateLobbyWatch() {
  const want = state === 'menu' && !NET.on && menuScreen() === 'online' && !!relayBase() && document.visibilityState === 'visible';
  LW.want = want;
  // short delay, so opening an invite link (which jumps straight into a room) never starts a list connection
  if (want && !LW.ws && !LW.pending) LW.pending = setTimeout(() => { LW.pending = 0; if (LW.want && !LW.ws) lobbyConnect(); }, 300);
  if (!want && LW.ws) {
    const ws = LW.ws; LW.ws = null;
    if (ws.readyState === 0) ws.onopen = () => { try { ws.close(1000); } catch (e) {} };
    else { try { ws.close(1000); } catch (e) {} }
  }
  renderRooms();
}
function lobbyConnect() {
  let ws;
  try { ws = new WebSocket(`${relayBase()}/lobby`); } catch (e) { LW.failed = true; renderRooms(); return; }
  LW.ws = ws; renderRooms();
  ws.onopen = () => { LW.retry = 0; LW.failed = false; renderRooms(); };
  ws.onmessage = e => {
    if (e.data === 'pong') return;
    let m; try { m = JSON.parse(e.data); } catch (err) { return; }
    if (m.t === 'rooms' && Array.isArray(m.rooms)) { LW.rooms = m.rooms; LW.off = (Number(m.now) || Date.now()) - Date.now(); LW.loaded = true; renderRooms(); }
  };
  ws.onclose = () => {
    if (LW.ws === ws) LW.ws = null;
    if (!LW.want) return;
    LW.failed = LW.retry > 1; renderRooms();
    clearTimeout(LW.timer); LW.timer = setTimeout(() => { if (LW.want && !LW.ws) lobbyConnect(); }, Math.min(10000, 1000 * Math.pow(2, LW.retry++)));
  };
}
setInterval(() => { if (LW.ws && LW.ws.readyState === 1) LW.ws.send('ping'); }, 25000);
setInterval(() => { if (LW.want) renderRooms(); }, 30000);
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => { updateLobbyWatch(); if (!document.hidden && state === 'menu') updateRejoin(); });
function renderRooms() {
  const el = $('#roomList'), live = $('#roomsLive');
  const note = t => { el.innerHTML = ''; const d = document.createElement('div'); d.className = 'note'; d.textContent = t; el.appendChild(d); };
  if (!relayBase()) { live.textContent = ''; note('Add your relay address to config.js to see open rooms.'); return; }
  live.textContent = LW.ws && LW.ws.readyState === 1 ? 'Live' : LW.failed ? "Can't reach the relay" : 'Connecting…';
  if (!LW.rooms.length) { note(LW.loaded ? 'No open rooms right now. Create a listed room and it will show up here.' : 'Looking for open rooms…'); return; }
  el.innerHTML = '';
  const now = Date.now() + LW.off;
  for (const r of LW.rooms) {
    const row = document.createElement('div'); row.className = 'roomItem';
    const info = document.createElement('div');
    const n = document.createElement('div'); n.className = 'rn'; n.textContent = `${cleanName(r.name, 'Player')}'s room`;
    const mins = Math.max(0, Math.round((now - Number(r.at)) / 60000));
    const d = document.createElement('div'); d.className = 'rm'; d.textContent = `${MODE_NAME[r.mode] || '8-ball'}, ${mins < 1 ? 'just opened' : mins + ' min waiting'}`;
    info.append(n, d);
    const b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'btn'; b.textContent = 'Join';
    b.addEventListener('click', () => { ensureAudio(); sfx('ui'); startOnline(cleanCode(r.code)); });
    row.append(info, b); el.appendChild(row);
  }
}

$('#netCode').addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') $('#bJoin').click(); });
$('#bLobbyCancel').addEventListener('click', () => { sfx('ui'); clearRejoin(); toMenu(); });
$('#bCopyLink').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const link = inviteLink();
  try {
    if (navigator.share && matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)').matches) { await navigator.share({ title: 'Retro Rack', text: 'Play pool with me', url: link }); return; }
    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(link); lobbyStatus('Invite link copied. Send it to your friend.');
  } catch (e) { const i = $('#lobbyLink'); i.focus(); i.select(); try { document.execCommand('copy'); lobbyStatus('Invite link copied.'); } catch (e2) { lobbyStatus('Copy the link below and send it to your friend.'); } }
});
try { $('#netName').value = localStorage.getItem('retroRack.name') || ''; } catch (e) {}
// dev mode (see the settings section): checked as each letter is typed, so it's done before Create or Join is pressed
async function isDevWord(v) {
  if (!v || !(window.crypto && crypto.subtle)) return false;
  try { const h = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', new TextEncoder().encode('retroRack:' + v.trim().toLowerCase())); return [...new Uint8Array(h)].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('') === DEVW.print; }
  catch (e) { return false; }
}
function setDev(on, word) {
  DEV = on; DEV_MYTHIC = false; try { if (on) localStorage.setItem('retroRack.dev', '1'); else localStorage.removeItem('retroRack.dev'); localStorage.removeItem('retroRack.devEmpty'); } catch (e) {}
  keepDevWord(on ? word : ''); if (!on) IDEAS.list = [];   // the word unlocks the players' ideas (see "ideas")
  LOCK = on ? devLocker() : lockerRead() || LK.newLocker();
  checkLooks(); saveS(); applyLook(); applyFx(); sendLook(); refreshMenus();
  toast(on ? 'Dev mode on: every look, £100,000 and 50 of each case, in a test locker' : 'Dev mode off: your own locker is back', 'good');
  if (on) loadIdeas().then(ok => { if (ok) showPop(); });
}
$('#netName').addEventListener('input', async () => {
  const v = $('#netName').value; if (!(await isDevWord(v)) || $('#netName').value !== v) return;
  try { $('#netName').value = localStorage.getItem('retroRack.name') || ''; } catch (e) { $('#netName').value = ''; }
  setDev(!DEV, v.trim());
});
$('#sDev').addEventListener('click', () => { sfx('ui'); setDev(false); });
$('#sDevMythic').addEventListener('click', () => { DEV_MYTHIC = DEV && !DEV_MYTHIC; sfx('ui'); refreshMenus(); });
// empty the test locker (a fresh one with no looks and no rewards, £100,000 and 50 of each case), or fill it again
$('#sDevEmpty').addEventListener('click', () => {
  if (!DEV) return; const empty = !devEmpty();
  if (empty && !confirm('Empty the test locker? It starts again with no looks and no rewards, £100,000 and 50 of each case, so cases behave as they do for a new player. Your own locker isn\'t touched.')) return;
  sfx('ui'); try { if (empty) localStorage.setItem('retroRack.devEmpty', '1'); else localStorage.removeItem('retroRack.devEmpty'); } catch (e) {}
  LOCK = devLocker(empty ? null : lockerRead());
  checkLooks(); saveS(); applyLook(); applyFx(); sendLook(); refreshMenus();
});

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ ideas
// Players send ideas to the game's maker: up to IDEA_MAX characters, with their online name and the game's version,
// kept by the relay (Ideas in relay/src/index.js, which also sets the limits). The lightbulb beside the money counter
// opens the box. In dev mode it opens the owner's inbox instead: the relay only hands the ideas over for the dev word,
// which this page keeps on this device (retroRack.devWord) from when it switched dev mode on, or from the inbox's own
// box. In dev mode the page asks for them once at start-up (nothing is polled), and a note under the corner icons and
// a number on the lightbulb say how many are new since the newest one read (retroRack.ideasSeen).
const IDEA_MAX = 100, IDEAS = { list: [], seen: 0, shown: 0, busy: false, pop: 0 };
try { IDEAS.seen = +localStorage.getItem('retroRack.ideasSeen') || 0; } catch (e) {}
const ideaUrl = () => { const b = relayBase(); return b ? b.replace(/^ws/, 'http') + '/ideas' : ''; };
const devWord = () => { try { return localStorage.getItem('retroRack.devWord') || ''; } catch (e) { return ''; } };
function keepDevWord(w) { try { if (w) localStorage.setItem('retroRack.devWord', w); else localStorage.removeItem('retroRack.devWord'); } catch (e) {} }
async function ideaPost(body) {   // refusals come back as { ok: false, why }, so nothing reaches the console
  try { const r = await fetch(ideaUrl(), { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'text/plain' }, body: JSON.stringify(body) }); return (await r.json()) || { ok: false, why: 'net' }; }
  catch (e) { return { ok: false, why: 'net' }; }
}
const IDEA_WHY = { busy: 'The idea box is full for today. Try again tomorrow.', long: `That's too long: ${IDEA_MAX} characters at most.`, empty: 'Write your idea first.',
  net: "Couldn't reach the game's server. Try again later.", key: "The server didn't take the dev word. Type it again." };
const ideaName = () => cleanName($('#netName').value, '');
const freshIdeas = () => IDEAS.list.filter(i => i.t > IDEAS.seen).length;
function ideaBadge() {
  const b = $('#bIdea'), n = DEV ? freshIdeas() : 0, lab = DEV ? 'Players\' ideas' : 'Send an idea';
  b.hidden = !ideaUrl(); b.title = lab; b.querySelector('.vh').textContent = lab + (n ? `: ${n} new` : '');
  $('#ideaN').hidden = !n; $('#ideaN').textContent = n > 99 ? '99+' : String(n);
  if (!n || !DEV) hidePop();
}
function showPop() {
  const n = freshIdeas(), p = $('#ideaPop'); if (!n || !DEV) return;
  p.textContent = `${n} new ${n === 1 ? 'idea' : 'ideas'} from players`; p.hidden = false;
  clearTimeout(IDEAS.pop); IDEAS.pop = setTimeout(hidePop, 12000);
}
function hidePop() { clearTimeout(IDEAS.pop); $('#ideaPop').hidden = true; }
function ideaLeft() {
  const t = $('#ideaIn').value; $('#ideaLeft').textContent = `${IDEA_MAX - t.length} left`;
  $('#bIdeaSend').disabled = !t.trim() || IDEAS.busy;
}
function openIdea() {
  if (DEV) { openIdeas(); return; }
  const name = ideaName();
  $('#ideaFrom').textContent = name ? `Sent with your online name, ${name}.` : 'Sent without a name: set one on the Online screen.';
  $('#ideaNote').textContent = ''; $('#bIdeaClose').textContent = 'Cancel'; ideaLeft();
  $('#idea').hidden = false; $('#ideaIn').focus({ preventScroll: true }); armBack();
}
function closeIdeaBox() {
  const was = !$('#idea').hidden || !$('#ideas').hidden; $('#idea').hidden = $('#ideas').hidden = true;
  if (was && state === 'menu' && NAV.stack.length < 2 && !paused) disarm();
}
async function sendIdea() {
  const text = $('#ideaIn').value.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); if (!text || IDEAS.busy) return;
  IDEAS.busy = true; ideaLeft(); $('#ideaNote').textContent = 'Sending…';
  const r = await ideaPost({ op: 'send', text, name: ideaName(), v: BUILD });
  IDEAS.busy = false;
  if (r.ok) { $('#ideaIn').value = ''; $('#bIdeaClose').textContent = 'Close'; sfx('ui'); }
  $('#ideaNote').textContent = r.ok ? `Thanks! Your idea is on its way.${r.left === 0 ? ' That was your last one for today.' : ''}`
    : r.why === 'limit' ? `You've sent ${r.max || 5} today, the most for one day. Try again tomorrow.` : IDEA_WHY[r.why] || IDEA_WHY.net;
  ideaLeft();
}
const ideaDate = t => new Date(t).toLocaleString('en-GB', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
// the inbox: fetches the ideas (with the dev word), newest first; the ones new since last time are marked
async function loadIdeas() {
  if (!DEV || !devWord() || !ideaUrl()) return false;
  const r = await ideaPost({ op: 'list', key: devWord() });
  if (r.ok) IDEAS.list = Array.isArray(r.ideas) ? r.ideas : [];
  else if (r.why === 'key') { keepDevWord(''); IDEAS.list = []; }
  ideaBadge(); if (!$('#ideas').hidden) renderIdeas(r.ok ? '' : IDEA_WHY[r.why] || IDEA_WHY.net);
  return r.ok;
}
function markRead() {
  IDEAS.seen = Math.max(IDEAS.seen, ...IDEAS.list.map(i => +i.t || 0));
  try { localStorage.setItem('retroRack.ideasSeen', String(IDEAS.seen)); } catch (e) {}
  ideaBadge();
}
function renderIdeas(note) {
  const key = devWord(), list = $('#ideasList'), n = IDEAS.list.length, fresh = IDEAS.list.filter(i => i.t > IDEAS.shown).length;
  $('#ideasUnlock').hidden = !!key; list.innerHTML = '';
  $('#ideasHead').textContent = !key ? 'Type your dev word to read them. It stays on this device.' : n ? `${n} ${n === 1 ? 'idea' : 'ideas'}${fresh ? `, ${fresh} new` : ''}, newest first.` : 'No ideas yet.';
  for (const it of IDEAS.list) {
    const row = mk('div', 'ideaRow' + (it.t > IDEAS.shown ? ' new' : '')), del = mk('button', 'btn', 'Delete'); del.type = 'button';
    del.addEventListener('click', async () => {
      sfx('ui'); const r = await ideaPost({ op: 'delete', key: devWord(), id: it.id });
      if (r.ok) IDEAS.list = IDEAS.list.filter(i => i.id !== it.id);
      renderIdeas(r.ok ? '' : IDEA_WHY[r.why] || IDEA_WHY.net); ideaBadge();
    });
    row.append(mk('div', 'ideaText', it.text), mk('div', 'ideaSub', `${it.name || 'No name'} · ${ideaDate(it.t)}${it.v ? ` · version ${it.v}` : ''}`), del);
    list.append(row);
  }
  $('#bIdeasClear').disabled = !n; $('#bIdeasRefresh').disabled = !key;
  $('#ideasNote').textContent = note || '';
}
async function openIdeas() {
  IDEAS.shown = IDEAS.seen; hidePop();
  $('#ideas').hidden = false; armBack();
  renderIdeas(devWord() ? 'Fetching the ideas…' : '');
  if (devWord()) { if (await loadIdeas()) markRead(); } else $('#ideasKey').focus({ preventScroll: true });
}
$('#bIdea').addEventListener('click', () => { if (state !== 'menu' || !$('#reel').hidden) return; ensureAudio(); sfx('ui'); openIdea(); });
$('#ideaPop').addEventListener('click', () => { ensureAudio(); sfx('ui'); openIdeas(); });
addEventListener('pointerdown', e => { if (!$('#ideaPop').hidden && !e.target.closest('#ideaPop')) hidePop(); }, true);   // a tap anywhere else puts the note away
$('#ideaIn').addEventListener('input', ideaLeft);
$('#ideaIn').addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); sendIdea(); } });   // one line: Enter sends
$('#bIdeaSend').addEventListener('click', () => { sfx('ui'); sendIdea(); });
for (const id of ['#bIdeaClose', '#bIdeasClose']) $(id).addEventListener('click', () => { sfx('ui'); closeIdeaBox(); });
for (const id of ['#idea', '#ideas']) {
  $(id).addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Escape') { e.stopPropagation(); closeIdeaBox(); } });
  let out = false;   // a tap outside the panel closes it, if the press started outside too
  $(id).addEventListener('pointerdown', e => { out = e.target === $(id); });
  $(id).addEventListener('click', e => { if (out && e.target === $(id)) closeIdeaBox(); });
}
$('#bIdeasRefresh').addEventListener('click', async () => { sfx('ui'); $('#ideasNote').textContent = 'Fetching the ideas…'; IDEAS.shown = IDEAS.seen; if (await loadIdeas()) markRead(); });
$('#bIdeasClear').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  if (!IDEAS.list.length || !confirm(`Delete all ${IDEAS.list.length} ideas? This can't be undone.`)) return;
  const r = await ideaPost({ op: 'clear', key: devWord() }); if (r.ok) IDEAS.list = [];
  renderIdeas(r.ok ? 'All deleted.' : IDEA_WHY[r.why] || IDEA_WHY.net); ideaBadge();
});
async function unlockIdeas() {
  const w = $('#ideasKey').value.trim(); if (!w) return;
  if (!(await isDevWord(w))) { $('#ideasNote').textContent = "That's not the dev word."; return; }
  keepDevWord(w); $('#ideasKey').value = ''; IDEAS.shown = IDEAS.seen; renderIdeas('Fetching the ideas…');
  if (await loadIdeas()) markRead();
}
$('#bIdeasKey').addEventListener('click', () => { sfx('ui'); unlockIdeas(); });
$('#ideasKey').addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') unlockIdeas(); });

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ installable app
// Only on a real web address: a downloaded copy opened from disk can't install or use a service worker.
$('#ver').textContent = `Version ${BUILD}${DEV ? ' · DEV' : ''}`;
if (/^https?:$/.test(location.protocol)) {
  const link = document.createElement('link'); link.rel = 'manifest'; link.href = 'manifest.webmanifest';
  const icon = document.createElement('link'); icon.rel = 'apple-touch-icon'; icon.href = 'icons/icon-192.png';
  document.head.append(link, icon);
  if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) navigator.serviceWorker.register('sw.js').catch(() => { /* the game works without it */ });
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ phone and screen comforts
// Vibration: only Android lets web pages vibrate, so the setting only shows on touch screens that offer it.
const canVibrate = 'vibrate' in navigator && matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)').matches;
function buzz(pattern) {
  if (!canVibrate || !S.vibrate || replay || TRK.demo) return;
  if (navigator.userActivation && !navigator.userActivation.hasBeenActive) return;   // refused (with a console warning) before the first tap
  try { navigator.vibrate(pattern); } catch (e) {}
}
$('#rowVibrate').hidden = !canVibrate;

// Fullscreen: iPhones don't allow it for web pages, so the buttons only show where the browser does
const canFull = !!(document.fullscreenEnabled && document.documentElement.requestFullscreen);
function toggleFull() {
  if (document.fullscreenElement) document.exitFullscreen().catch(() => {});
  else document.documentElement.requestFullscreen({ navigationUI: 'hide' }).catch(() => {});
}
// pixel-art icons, drawn from these rows (x = a pixel): the menu's corner buttons, and on phones the game's buttons
const ICONS = {
  cam: ['......xxx.', '.....xxxxx', '.....xxxxx', '...x.xxxxx', '..xx..xxx.', '.xx.......', 'xx........', 'x.........'],
  top: ['xx..xx..xx', 'xxxxxxxxxx', '.x......x.', '.x......x.', '.x......x.', '.x......x.', 'xxxxxxxxxx', 'xx..xx..xx'],
  help: ['..xxxxx...', '.xx...xx..', '......xx..', '.....xx...', '....xx....', '....xx....', '..........', '....xx....', '....xx....'],
  pause: ['.xxx..xxx.', '.xxx..xxx.', '.xxx..xxx.', '.xxx..xxx.', '.xxx..xxx.', '.xxx..xxx.', '.xxx..xxx.', '.xxx..xxx.'],
  replay: ['...xxxx.x.', '.xx....xx.', 'x.....xxx.', 'x.........', 'x........x', 'x........x', '.x......x.', '..xxxxxx..'],
  chat: ['.xxxxxxxx.', 'xxxxxxxxxx', 'xx.xx.xx.x', 'xxxxxxxxxx', '.xxxxxxxx.', '..xx......', '..x.......'],
  rack: ['....xx....', '....xx....', '..xx..xx..', '..xx..xx..', 'xx..xx..xx', 'xx..xx..xx'],
  undo: ['...x......', '..xx......', '.xxxxxxx..', '..xx....x.', '...x.....x', '.........x', '........x.', '....xxxx..'],
  hand: ['...x.x....', '.x.x.x.x..', '.x.x.x.x..', '.x.x.x.x..', '.xxxxxxx.x', '.xxxxxxxxx', '.xxxxxxxx.', '..xxxxxx..', '...xxxx...'],
  move: ['....xx....', '...xxxx...', '....xx....', '.x..xx..x.', 'xxxxxxxxxx', 'xxxxxxxxxx', '.x..xx..x.', '....xx....', '...xxxx...', '....xx....'],
  addball: ['..xxx.....', '.xxxxx....', 'xxxxxxx...', 'xxxxxxx...', 'xxxxxxx.x.', '.xxxxx.xxx', '..xxx...x.'],
  save: ['xxxxxxxxx.', 'x.xxxxx.xx', 'x.xxxxx..x', 'x.xxxxx..x', 'x........x', 'x.xxxxxx.x', 'x.x....x.x', 'xxxxxxxxxx'],
  left: ['...xx', '..xx.', '.xx..', 'xx...', '.xx..', '..xx.', '...xx'],
  right: ['xx...', '.xx..', '..xx.', '...xx', '..xx.', '.xx..', 'xx...'],
  eye: ['...xxxx...', '.xx....xx.', 'x...xx...x', 'x..xxxx..x', 'x...xx...x', '.xx....xx.', '...xxxx...'],
  bin: ['...xxxx...', 'xxxxxxxxxx', '.x......x.', '.x.x..x.x.', '.x.x..x.x.', '.x.x..x.x.', '.x......x.', '..xxxxxx..'],
  slow: ['xxxxxxxx', '.x....x.', '..x..x..', '...xx...', '..x..x..', '.x.xx.x.', 'xxxxxxxx'],
  play: ['x.....', 'xxx...', 'xxxxx.', 'xxxxxx', 'xxxxx.', 'xxx...', 'x.....'],
  skip: ['x....x', 'xx...x', 'xxx..x', 'xxxx.x', 'xxx..x', 'xx...x', 'x....x'],
  pumpkin: ['....xx....', '..xxxxxx..', '.xxxxxxxx.', 'xx..xx..xx', 'xxxxxxxxxx', 'xx.x..x.xx', 'xxx....xxx', '.xxxxxxxx.', '..xxxxxx..'],
  bulb: ['...xxxx...', '..x....x..', '.x..xx..x.', '.x.x..x.x.', '.x......x.', '..x....x..', '...x..x...', '...xxxx...', '...xxxx...', '....xx....'],   // ideas
  coin: ['...xxxx...', '.xxxxxxxx.', '.xxxx..xx.', 'xxxx.xxxxx', 'xxx...xxxx', 'xxxx.xxxxx', 'xxxx.xxxxx', '.xx....xx.', '.xxxxxxxx.', '...xxxx...'],   // a pound coin
  gear: ['...xxxx...', '.x.xxxx.x.', '..xxxxxx..', 'xxxx..xxxx', 'xxx....xxx', 'xxx....xxx', 'xxxx..xxxx', '..xxxxxx..', '.x.xxxx.x.', '...xxxx...'],
  full: ['xxx....xxx', 'x........x', 'x........x', '..........', '..........', '..........', '..........', 'x........x', 'x........x', 'xxx....xxx'],
  unfull: ['..x....x..', '..x....x..', 'xxx....xxx', '..........', '..........', '..........', '..........', 'xxx....xxx', '..x....x..', '..x....x..'],
};
function setIcon(btn, name) {
  const rows = ICONS[name], cells = [];
  rows.forEach((r, y) => { for (let x = 0; x < r.length; x++) if (r[x] === 'x') cells.push(`<rect x="${x}" y="${y}" width="1" height="1"/>`); });
  const old = btn.querySelector('svg'); if (old) old.remove();
  btn.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', `<svg viewBox="0 0 ${rows[0].length} ${rows.length}" fill="currentColor" aria-hidden="true">${cells.join('')}</svg>`);
}
setIcon($('#bMenuSettings'), 'gear'); setIcon($('#bMoney'), 'coin'); setIcon($('#bIdea'), 'bulb');
// On phones the game's buttons are small squares with an icon; their names show on a computer, and on a phone a long
// press shows one. A button's name (label) can change: setLab keeps its words, icon and aria-label together.
function setLab(b, label, icon) { const l = b.querySelector('.lab'); if (l) l.textContent = label; else b.textContent = label; b.setAttribute('aria-label', label); if (icon) setIcon(b, icon); }
for (const [q, icon, name] of [['#bCam', 'cam'], ['#bTop', 'top'], ['#bHelp', 'help'], ['#bPause', 'pause'], ['#bFull', 'full'], ['#bReplay', 'replay'], ['#bChat', 'chat'],
  ['#bRerack', 'rack'], ['#bUndo', 'undo'], ['#bHand', 'hand'], ['#bMove', 'move'], ['#bBalls', 'addball'], ['#bSave', 'save'], ['#bAimL', 'left', 'Turn aim left'], ['#bAimR', 'right', 'Turn aim right'],
  ['#bTrickPrev', 'left', 'Previous trick'], ['#bTrickNext', 'right', 'Next trick'], ['#bShowMe', 'eye'], ['#bDelLayout', 'bin'], ['#bSlow', 'slow'], ['#bSkip', 'skip']]) {
  const b = $(q), words = b.textContent; b.textContent = ''; b.classList.add('ib'); b.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<span class="lab"></span>');
  setLab(b, words, icon); if (name) b.setAttribute('aria-label', name);
}
const LP = { t: 0, b: null, fired: false };   // a long press on an icon button (not the aim arrows, which turn while held)
addEventListener('pointerdown', e => {
  const b = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.ib'); LP.fired = false; clearTimeout(LP.t);
  if (!b || !phoneUI.matches || b.closest('#aimRow')) return;
  LP.b = b; LP.t = setTimeout(() => {
    LP.fired = true; const t = $('#ibTip'), r = b.getBoundingClientRect(); t.textContent = b.getAttribute('aria-label'); t.hidden = false;
    t.style.left = Math.max(4, Math.min(innerWidth - t.offsetWidth - 4, r.left + r.width / 2 - t.offsetWidth / 2)) + 'px';
    t.style.top = (r.top > t.offsetHeight + 12 ? r.top - t.offsetHeight - 6 : r.bottom + 6) + 'px';
  }, 450);
}, true);
for (const ev of ['pointerup', 'pointercancel']) addEventListener(ev, () => { clearTimeout(LP.t); if (LP.fired) setTimeout(() => { $('#ibTip').hidden = true; }, 900); }, true);
addEventListener('click', e => { if (LP.fired && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.ib') === LP.b) { e.stopImmediatePropagation(); e.preventDefault(); LP.fired = false; } }, true);   // the name, not the button
addEventListener('contextmenu', e => { if (e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.ib')) e.preventDefault(); });
// The Controls panel's keys can be tapped, each doing what the key does. Views, shots and the practice keys close the
// panel first, so you see what happens; the ones that adjust the shot (aim, power, spin) leave it open for more taps.
const KEY_OF = { '←': 'ArrowLeft', '→': 'ArrowRight', '↑': 'ArrowUp', '↓': 'ArrowDown', Space: 'Space' }, KEY_STAY = /^(Arrow|Key[ADWSIJKLO]$)/;
for (const kb of document.querySelectorAll('#help kbd')) {
  const t = kb.textContent.trim(), k = KEY_OF[t] || (/^[A-Z]$/.test(t) ? 'Key' + t : null); if (!k) continue;   // Shift, Ctrl and Alt only go with other keys
  const ctrl = !!kb.closest('[data-ctrl]');   // the arrows written with Ctrl do the fine step
  kb.classList.add('tap'); kb.setAttribute('role', 'button'); kb.tabIndex = 0; kb.dataset.k = k; if (ctrl) kb.dataset.ctrl = '';
  kb.addEventListener('click', () => tapKey(k, ctrl));
  kb.addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.code === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); tapKey(k, ctrl); } });
}
function tapKey(k, ctrl) {
  ensureAudio(); if (!KEY_STAY.test(k)) $('#help').hidden = true;
  if (state === 'menu' || state === 'lobby' || paused) return;
  if (k === 'KeyV') { if (replay) endReplay(); else startReplay(); return; }
  if (state === 'over') return;
  if (k === 'KeyQ' || k === 'KeyE') { cam.orbit(k === 'KeyE' ? 70 : -70, 0); return; }   // a turn of about 25°
  if (/^(Arrow(Up|Down)|Key[WS])$/.test(k) && !ctrl) { if (state === 'aim' && humanTurn()) { aim.power = clamp(aim.power + (k === 'ArrowUp' || k === 'KeyW' ? 0.025 : -0.025), 0.02, 1); aimDirty = true; updateShotPanel(); } return; }
  keyAct(k, false, ctrl);
}
function updateFullBtns() {
  const on = !!document.fullscreenElement, word = on ? 'Exit fullscreen' : 'Fullscreen';
  $('#bFull').hidden = !canFull; setLab($('#bFull'), word, on ? 'unfull' : 'full'); $('#bFull').setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(on));
  const m = $('#bMenuFull'); m.hidden = !canFull; m.title = word; m.querySelector('.vh').textContent = word; m.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(on)); setIcon(m, on ? 'unfull' : 'full');
}
$('#bFull').addEventListener('click', () => { sfx('ui'); toggleFull(); });
$('#bMenuFull').addEventListener('click', () => { ensureAudio(); sfx('ui'); toggleFull(); });
document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', updateFullBtns);
updateFullBtns();

// "Turn your phone sideways": only on a phone held upright; goes when it's rotated, and OK hides it for good
const portraitPhone = matchMedia('(pointer: coarse) and (orientation: portrait) and (max-width: 600px)');
let rotateOff = false;
try { rotateOff = localStorage.getItem('retroRack.rotateHint') === 'off'; } catch (e) {}
function updateRotateHint() { $('#rotate').hidden = rotateOff || !portraitPhone.matches; }
portraitPhone.addEventListener('change', updateRotateHint);
$('#bRotateOk').addEventListener('click', () => { rotateOff = true; try { localStorage.setItem('retroRack.rotateHint', 'off'); } catch (e) {} updateRotateHint(); });
updateRotateHint();

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ main loop
let lastT = performance.now();
function frame(now) {
  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  const dt = Math.min(0.05, (now - lastT) / 1000); lastT = now;
  if (!paused) {
    handleKeys(dt);
    if (state === 'moving') physicsTick(dt);
    else if (state === 'set') setTick(dt);
    else if (state === 'botThink') botThinkTick(now);
    else if (state === 'botAim') botAimTick(dt);
    potAnimTick(dt);
  }
  netTick(now, dt);
  updateBalls();
  updateCue(paused ? 0 : dt);
  if (aimDirty) updateAimTxt();
  updateGuides(now);
  updateMarkers(now); clothFx(now); tableFx(now, paused ? 0 : dt); hallFx(now);
  if (state !== 'aim') { G_CUE.visible = G_CUE2.visible = G_OBJ.visible = G_GHOST.visible = false; }
  if (cam.mode === 'shot' && state !== 'set' && state !== 'stroke' && state !== 'moving') cam.shotOver();
  cam.update(dt);
  renderer.setRenderTarget(rt); renderer.render(scene, camera);
  renderer.setRenderTarget(null); renderer.render(postScene, postCam);
}
addEventListener('resize', resize);
resize(); applyLook(); syncBallMeshes(); refreshMenus(); updateCamButtons();
requestAnimationFrame(frame);
{ // opening an invite link joins the room straight away
  const m = /#room=([A-Za-z0-9]+)/.exec(location.hash);
  if (m) { const code = cleanCode(m[1]); $('#netCode').value = code; M.opp = 'online'; if (M.mode === 'practice') M.mode = '8ball'; refreshMenus(); if (relayBase()) startOnline(code); else { menuReset(['home', 'multi', 'online']); menuNote('This is an invite link, but online play needs the relay address in config.js first.'); } }
}
// collection effects (see "collection effects"): their first drawing, and any reward a saved locker is owed
applyFx(); setTimeout(claimDue, 600);
ideaBadge(); if (DEV) loadIdeas().then(ok => { if (ok && state === 'menu') showPop(); });   // the players' ideas: once, at start-up
window.__rr = { get state() { return state; }, syncBallMeshes, get world() { return world; }, get game() { return game; }, NET, get replay() { return replay; },
  get matchWins() { return matchWins; }, CAR, LK, DEVW, get dev() { return DEV; }, concedeFrame, cheer, applyLook, get cheering() { return cheerGlove.visible ? CHEER.kind : ''; }, get lock() { return LOCK; }, get earn() { return EARN; }, get reel() { return REEL; },
  get fx() { return { cloth: clothTex ? clothTex.offset.x : 0, glow: clothMat.emissive.getHexString(), glove: GLM.base.emissive.getHexString(), show: FX.raf }; },
  get effects() { return { on: LK.effects(LOCK).filter(fxOn), rails: railMat.map !== woodTex, cosmic: BM[0].material.map === cosmicTex.t, stars: wallMat.map === starTex.t, hall: HALL.on, sign: signText(), trail: trailMesh.visible, sparks: SPK.parts.length }; }, lookRank, sparkAt,
  get look() { return { venue: VEN.key, sign: VEN.sign, cue: cueNow, cloth: PREV.on ? PREV.looks.cloth : NET.on && NET.cloth ? NET.cloth : S.cloth, glove: gloveNow, clothTex: !!clothMat.map, shown: cueMesh.visible, cam: cam.mode }; },
  ballScreen(id) { const b = world.balls.find(x => x.id === id); const v = new THREE.Vector3(b.x, R, b.z).project(camera); const r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect(); return [r.left + (v.x + 1) / 2 * r.width, r.top + (1 - v.y) / 2 * r.height]; }, aim, cam, startGame, M, S, beginStroke, shoot, toggleTop, toggleAimCam,
  get hands() {   // the gloves at the table: one piece on a skeleton or built from parts, and how far the cue hand's fingertips are from the middle of the cue (cm)
    const info = root => { let sk = null, parts = 0; root.traverse(o => { if (o.isSkinnedMesh) sk = o; else if (o.isMesh) parts++; }); return { skinned: !!sk, bones: sk ? sk.skeleton.bones.length : 0, parts, geo: sk ? sk.geometry.uuid : '' }; };
    const g = gripGlove.children[0], v = new THREE.Vector3(); let tips = [];
    if (g) g.traverse(o => { if (o.isSkinnedMesh) tips = [4, 7, 10, 13].map(i => { v.set(0, 0, -0.018); o.skeleton.bones[i].localToWorld(v); g.worldToLocal(v); return Math.round(Math.hypot(v.y, v.z) * 1000) / 10; }); });
    return { grip: { ...info(gripGlove), tips }, bridge: info(bridgeGlove), textured: !!GLM.base.map };
  },
  tableFrame() { const v = new THREE.Vector3(); let m = 0; for (const [x, z] of [[1, 1], [1, -1], [-1, 1], [-1, -1]]) { v.set(x * (T.hl + 0.2), 0, z * (T.hw + 0.2)).project(camera); m = Math.max(m, Math.abs(v.x), Math.abs(v.y)); } return m; },   // how far the table's corners reach across the screen (1: the edge)
  marked() { return MK.map((k, id) => k.visible ? id : -1).filter(id => id >= 0); } };
})();
```

## src/shell.html

Page markup and CSS. The build fills in the fonts, favicon and code (__PS2P__, __VT323__, __FAVICON__, __BUNDLE__). (661 lines)

```html
<!doctype html>
<html lang="en-GB">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<script>window.RETRO_RACK_RELAY = "";</script>
<!-- Online play: the relay address lives in config.js, next to this file. -->
<script src="config.js"></script>
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover">
<title>Retro Rack: 3D pixel pool</title>
<meta name="theme-color" content="#1a1433">
<link rel="icon" type="image/png" href="data:image/png;base64,__FAVICON__">
<style>
@font-face{font-family:'Press Start 2P';src:url(data:font/woff2;base64,__PS2P__) format('woff2');font-display:block}
@font-face{font-family:'VT323';src:url(data:font/woff2;base64,__VT323__) format('woff2');font-display:block}
:root{--night:#1a1433;--plum:#3a2452;--plum2:#4e3270;--lamp:#ffc56b;--baize:#1d8a74;--chalk:#6cb8ff;--rose:#ff6f8f;--paper:#f7ead2;--ink:#0d0a1c}
*{box-sizing:border-box}
html,body{margin:0;height:100%;overflow:hidden;background:var(--night);color:var(--paper);font-family:'VT323',ui-monospace,monospace;font-size:22px;line-height:1.15;-webkit-user-select:none;user-select:none;touch-action:none;-webkit-tap-highlight-color:transparent}
[hidden]{display:none!important}
#view{position:fixed;left:0;top:0;display:block;image-rendering:pixelated;image-rendering:crisp-edges;cursor:crosshair}
#scan{position:fixed;inset:0;pointer-events:none;display:none;background:repeating-linear-gradient(to bottom,rgba(0,0,0,.2) 0 1px,transparent 1px 3px)}
body.scan #scan{display:block}
.panel{background:rgba(22,17,44,.9);border:3px solid var(--lamp);box-shadow:6px 6px 0 var(--ink)}
.btn{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:10px;line-height:1.35;color:var(--paper);background:var(--plum);border:3px solid var(--ink);border-radius:0;box-shadow:inset -3px -3px 0 rgba(0,0,0,.35),inset 3px 3px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.1);padding:9px 11px;cursor:pointer}
.btn:hover{background:var(--plum2)}
.btn:active{transform:translate(2px,2px)}
.btn[aria-pressed="true"]{background:var(--lamp);color:var(--ink)}
.btn:disabled{opacity:.45;cursor:default;transform:none}
button:focus-visible{outline:3px dashed var(--chalk);outline-offset:2px}
kbd{font-family:'VT323',monospace;font-size:19px;line-height:1;background:var(--plum);border:2px solid var(--ink);padding:0 5px;margin:0 1px;color:var(--paper);box-shadow:inset -2px -2px 0 rgba(0,0,0,.35)}

/* shared by the menus and the panels (settings, game over, waiting room, offers) */
.row{display:grid;grid-template-columns:104px 1fr;gap:6px 14px;align-items:start;margin:0 0 12px}
.lbl{color:var(--lamp);font-size:22px;padding-top:6px}
.seg{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px}
.note{grid-column:2;font-size:20px;opacity:.82;max-width:46ch}
.swatches{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap}
.swatch{width:34px;height:34px;border:3px solid var(--ink);border-radius:0;cursor:pointer;box-shadow:inset -3px -3px 0 rgba(0,0,0,.3)}
.swatch[aria-pressed="true"]{outline:3px solid var(--lamp);outline-offset:2px}
.actions{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:20px}
/* menu: a stack of screens. Moving between them, the whole panel glides across the screen: the old one
   leaves one side as the new one arrives from the other, like slides on a strip (see menuSlide in game.js) */
#menu{position:fixed;inset:0;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:flex-start;padding:3vh 4vw;pointer-events:none;overflow:hidden}
#menuPanel{pointer-events:auto;width:min(660px,94vw);max-height:94vh;display:flex;flex-direction:column;overflow:hidden;padding:0}
.mGhost{position:fixed;z-index:3;pointer-events:none;margin:0}
.mTop{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:14px;padding:14px 20px 0;flex:none}
#mTitle{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-weight:400;font-size:12px;line-height:1.4;color:var(--lamp);margin:0}
#mStage{display:grid;overflow:auto;min-height:0;padding:16px 20px 14px}
.mScreen{grid-area:1 / 1;min-width:0}
.title{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-weight:400;font-size:clamp(26px,4.2vw,42px);line-height:1.15;color:var(--lamp);margin:0 0 16px;text-shadow:3px 3px 0 var(--rose),6px 6px 0 var(--plum2),9px 9px 0 var(--ink)}
.cards{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(250px,1fr));gap:10px}
.card{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;text-align:left;padding:10px 12px;font:inherit;color:var(--paper);background:var(--plum);border:3px solid var(--ink);border-radius:0;cursor:pointer;box-shadow:inset -3px -3px 0 rgba(0,0,0,.35),inset 3px 3px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.1),4px 4px 0 var(--ink);transition:transform .12s,background .12s,border-color .12s}
.card:hover{background:var(--plum2);border-color:var(--lamp);transform:translate(-1px,-2px)}
.card:active{transform:translate(2px,2px);box-shadow:inset -3px -3px 0 rgba(0,0,0,.35),inset 3px 3px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.1),2px 2px 0 var(--ink)}
.card[aria-pressed="true"]{border-color:var(--lamp)}
.cardArt{height:32px;width:auto;image-rendering:pixelated;flex:none}
.cardBody{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;min-width:0}
.cardName{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:11px;line-height:1.3;color:var(--lamp);white-space:nowrap}
.cardTxt{font-size:18px;line-height:1;opacity:.72}
.cardTxt:empty{display:none}
#bStart,#bQuick,#bCreate,#bCareer{font-size:12px;padding:12px 16px;background:var(--rose);color:var(--ink)}
#bStart:hover,#bQuick:hover,#bCreate:hover,#bCareer:hover{background:#ff8ba5}
.quickRow{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px}.quickRow>.btn{margin-bottom:12px;text-align:left}
#bRejoin{font-size:12px;padding:12px 16px;background:var(--lamp);color:var(--ink)}#bRejoin:hover{background:#ffd48e}
.mFoot{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;flex:none;padding:10px 20px 14px;border-top:3px solid var(--plum2)}
/* the money counter, Settings and Fullscreen: in the top-right corner while in the menus (inside the panel's corner on
   phones). They hide with the menu. While a case's reel is open the counter stays in view above it (the icons don't) */
#menuIcons{position:fixed;top:14px;right:16px;display:flex;gap:8px;pointer-events:auto;z-index:2}
#menu[hidden]~#menuIcons{display:none}
#reel:not([hidden])~#menuIcons{z-index:21;pointer-events:none}#reel:not([hidden])~#menuIcons :is(.iconBtn,#ideaPop){visibility:hidden}
.iconBtn{padding:7px;line-height:0}.iconBtn svg{width:22px;height:22px;shape-rendering:crispEdges}
#bMoney{position:relative;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;padding:7px 10px;line-height:0;font-size:12px;color:var(--lamp)}#bMoney svg{width:22px;height:22px;flex:none;shape-rendering:crispEdges}
#moneyAmt{line-height:1;white-space:nowrap;transition:color .3s}#bMoney.gain #moneyAmt{color:#8dffa0}#bMoney.spend #moneyAmt{color:var(--rose)}
#bMoney.dev{border-style:dashed;border-color:var(--chalk)}
/* ideas: the lightbulb (in dev mode with the number of new ones), the note under the icons, the box and the inbox */
#bIdea{position:relative}#ideaN{position:absolute;top:-7px;right:-7px;min-width:17px;padding:2px 3px;font-size:8px;line-height:1;text-align:center;background:var(--rose);color:var(--ink);border:2px solid var(--ink)}
#ideaPop{position:absolute;top:100%;right:0;margin-top:8px;font-size:9px;white-space:nowrap;background:var(--rose);color:var(--ink)}
#idea,#ideas{align-items:flex-start}   /* near the top, clear of a phone's keyboard */
.ideaPanel{width:min(460px,92vw);padding:16px 18px 14px}.ideaPanel h2{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-weight:400;font-size:12px;color:var(--lamp);margin:0 0 10px}
#ideaIn{display:block;width:100%;resize:none;line-height:1.1}
.ideaMeta{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;margin-top:5px;font-size:18px;opacity:.85}#ideaLeft{white-space:nowrap;color:var(--lamp)}
.ideaPanel .note{grid-column:auto;margin:6px 0 0;max-width:none}.ideaPanel .note:empty{display:none}.ideaPanel .actions{margin-top:10px}
.ideasPanel{width:min(620px,94vw)}#ideasHead{margin:0 0 6px;font-size:19px}#ideasUnlock{margin-bottom:6px}#ideasKey{width:180px}
#ideasList{max-height:calc(92vh - 190px);overflow-y:auto;padding-right:6px;scrollbar-color:var(--plum2) transparent}
.ideaRow{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr auto;gap:1px 10px;align-items:center;padding:5px 9px;margin:0 0 5px;background:var(--plum);border:3px solid var(--ink);border-left:8px solid var(--plum2)}
.ideaRow.new{border-left-color:var(--rose)}.ideaText{font-size:20px;line-height:1.05;overflow-wrap:anywhere;-webkit-user-select:text;user-select:text}.ideaSub{grid-column:1;font-size:15px;opacity:.75}
.ideaRow .btn{grid-row:1 / span 2;grid-column:2;font-size:8px;padding:5px 7px}.ideaNone{margin:4px 0;opacity:.75}
.moneyDelta{position:absolute;top:100%;right:0;margin-top:4px;padding:3px 6px;line-height:1;white-space:nowrap;background:var(--ink);pointer-events:none}.moneyDelta.up{color:#8dffa0}.moneyDelta.down{color:var(--rose)}
.vh{position:absolute;width:1px;height:1px;overflow:hidden;clip:rect(0 0 0 0);white-space:nowrap}
#ver{margin:12px 0 0;font-size:16px;opacity:.5}
/* career: the new-career form, the hub (you and your events) and an event (the draw and your next opponent) */
.portrait,.shopSw{image-rendering:pixelated;flex:none}
#cName{width:200px}
.cStyleBtn{padding:4px 6px;line-height:0}.cStyleBtn[aria-pressed="true"]{border-color:var(--lamp);background:var(--plum2)}
.cCols{gap:6px;align-items:center}.cCols .swatches{gap:5px;margin-right:10px}.cCols .swatch{width:24px;height:24px}.cColLbl{font-size:18px;opacity:.8}
.cTop{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:0 0 10px}
.cMe{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px}
.cMeName{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:12px;color:var(--lamp);margin-bottom:6px}
.cMeStats{font-size:20px}
.cEvents{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px}
.cTiers{margin:0 0 8px}.cTiers .btn.locked{opacity:.55}
.shopSw{width:30px;height:30px;border:3px solid var(--ink)}
/* the locker: cases, the inventory (one long list, rarest first) and the shop (for sale, then a row per collection).
   The lists scroll inside the panel, so the screen itself still fits a phone on its side */
.lockTop{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px 12px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:8px}.lockTop .cInfo{margin:0;flex:1 1 auto}
.lockList{max-height:calc(94vh - 238px);overflow-y:auto;overflow-x:hidden;padding-right:6px;scrollbar-color:var(--plum2) transparent}
.invHead{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:9px;margin:10px 0 5px}.invHead:first-child{margin-top:0}
.invRow{--rc:#8f86a8;display:grid;grid-template-columns:auto 1fr auto;grid-template-rows:auto auto;gap:1px 10px;align-items:center;width:100%;text-align:left;padding:5px 9px;margin:0 0 5px;font:inherit;color:var(--paper);background:var(--plum);border:3px solid var(--ink);border-left:8px solid var(--rc);border-radius:0;cursor:pointer;box-sizing:border-box}
.invRow:hover{background:var(--plum2)}.invRow[aria-pressed="true"]{border-color:var(--lamp);border-left-color:var(--rc)}
.invRow>img,.invRow>.fxIco{grid-row:1 / span 2}.invRow>img.portrait{max-height:30px;width:auto}
.invName{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:9px;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;min-width:0}
.invSub{grid-column:2 / span 2;font-size:16px;line-height:1;color:var(--rc)}.invState{font-size:17px;color:var(--lamp)}
.fxRow{cursor:default}.fxIco{width:30px;text-align:center;font-size:26px;color:var(--rc)}.fxRow .btn{font-size:9px;padding:5px 9px}.fxRow .invSub{color:var(--paper);opacity:.8}
.colHead{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:baseline;gap:2px 10px;margin:12px 0 6px}.colHead:first-child{margin-top:0}
.colHead b{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:9px;font-weight:400}.colN{font-size:18px;color:var(--lamp)}.colN.done{color:#8dffa0}
.colRw{flex-basis:100%;font-size:16px;line-height:1.05;opacity:.82}
.colRow{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px}
.colSub{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:baseline;gap:2px 10px;margin:7px 0 4px;padding-left:2px}.colKind{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:8px;opacity:.9}.colSub .colRw{flex-basis:auto}
.colTile{position:relative;width:58px;height:58px;padding:0;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:2px;align-items:center;justify-content:center;border:3px solid;border-radius:0;cursor:pointer;box-sizing:border-box}
.colTile>img.portrait{max-height:34px;width:auto}.colTile>.shopSw{width:30px;height:30px}
.colTile.locked{opacity:.6}.colTile.locked>img{filter:grayscale(.85) brightness(.6)}.colTile.sale{height:66px}.colTile:hover{filter:brightness(1.15)}
.colTile.trying{outline:3px dashed var(--lamp);outline-offset:1px}
.colTick{position:absolute;right:3px;bottom:0;font-size:17px;color:#8dffa0;text-shadow:1px 1px 0 var(--ink)}
.colPrice{font-size:14px;line-height:1;padding:0 3px;background:rgba(13,10,28,.78)}.colTile.sale>img.portrait{max-height:28px}.colTile.sale>.shopSw{width:26px;height:26px}
.caseGrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,minmax(0,1fr));gap:8px}
.caseGrid.hwCases{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr));margin:6px 0 10px}.hwHead .colRw{flex-basis:100%}
.caseCard{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:4px;padding:8px 6px;text-align:center;background:var(--plum);border:3px solid var(--ink);box-shadow:inset -3px -3px 0 rgba(0,0,0,.35),inset 3px 3px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.1),3px 3px 0 var(--ink)}
.caseName{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:9px;line-height:1.3}.caseN{font-size:18px;color:var(--lamp)}.caseOdds{font-size:15px;line-height:1;opacity:.8}
.caseBtns{display:flex;gap:4px;flex-wrap:wrap;justify-content:center;margin-top:auto}.caseBtns .btn{font-size:8px;padding:6px 7px}.caseBtns .btn.dim{opacity:.5}
.lockFoot{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;margin-top:8px;font-size:17px}#bPreview{margin-right:8px;color:var(--lamp)}
/* Preview: the menu steps aside; a tap anywhere comes back */
#preview{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:6;display:flex;align-items:flex-end;justify-content:center;gap:10px;padding:0 16px 4vh;cursor:pointer}
#prevTxt{max-width:min(720px,80vw);margin:0;padding:7px 12px;background:rgba(13,10,28,.82);border:3px solid var(--ink);font-size:20px;text-align:center}.lockFoot>span:first-child{margin-right:auto;opacity:.85}.lockFoot .btn{font-size:9px;padding:5px 9px}
#lockNote{grid-column:auto;margin:6px 0 0}#lockNote:empty{display:none}
.picker{align-items:center;flex-wrap:nowrap}.pickCur{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;min-width:170px;font-size:19px}.pickCur .shopSw{width:22px;height:22px;border-width:2px}
/* opening a case: the screen goes dark, a big reel of the case's items glides past a marker and stops on the prize,
   and the background (#reelFx, drawn in game.js) lights up in the prize's colours */
#reel{z-index:20;background:rgba(4,3,9,.92);-webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(3px);backdrop-filter:blur(3px);padding:0;flex-direction:column}
#reelFx{position:absolute;inset:0;width:100%;height:100%;image-rendering:pixelated;pointer-events:none}
.reelPanel{position:relative;width:100%;text-align:center;padding:0;--tile:min(40vh,22vw)}
#reelTitle{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-weight:400;font-size:clamp(14px,2.4vw,26px);color:var(--paper);margin:0 0 3vh;text-shadow:3px 3px 0 #05030a,-2px -2px 0 #05030a,0 0 14px currentColor}
#reelWin{position:relative;overflow:hidden;height:calc(var(--tile) + 28px);margin:0;border-top:4px solid var(--ink);border-bottom:4px solid var(--ink);background:rgba(13,10,28,.72);cursor:pointer}
#reelStrip{display:flex;gap:12px;padding:10px 0;will-change:transform}
.reelTile{flex:none;width:var(--tile);height:var(--tile);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;background:var(--plum);border:5px solid;box-sizing:border-box}
.reelTile img{width:66%;height:auto;max-height:80%;image-rendering:pixelated}.reelTile img.shopSw{width:62%;height:62%;border-width:4px}
.reelTile.won{outline:5px solid var(--lamp);box-shadow:0 0 28px var(--glow,var(--lamp));filter:brightness(1.12)}
.reelMark{position:absolute;top:0;bottom:0;left:50%;width:4px;margin-left:-2px;background:var(--lamp);box-shadow:0 0 10px var(--lamp)}
.reelMark::before,.reelMark::after{content:'';position:absolute;left:-8px;border:10px solid transparent}.reelMark::before{top:0;border-top-color:var(--lamp)}.reelMark::after{bottom:0;border-bottom-color:var(--lamp)}
#reelTxt{min-height:2.6em;margin:3vh auto 2vh;max-width:90vw;font-size:clamp(20px,2.6vw,34px);text-shadow:2px 2px 0 #05030a}
.reelCol{display:block;margin-top:8px;color:#8dffa0;font-size:.8em;text-shadow:2px 2px 0 #05030a}
.reelRarity{display:block;font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:clamp(13px,2vw,24px);margin-bottom:10px;text-shadow:3px 3px 0 #05030a,-2px -2px 0 #05030a,0 0 14px currentColor}
#reelBtns{justify-content:center}#reelBtns .btn{font-size:12px;padding:12px 16px}
.reelPanel.shake{animation:reelShake .6s linear}
@keyframes reelShake{0%,100%{transform:none}20%{transform:translate(-5px,2px)}40%{transform:translate(5px,-2px)}60%{transform:translate(-3px,-1px)}80%{transform:translate(3px,1px)}}
#reelTitle.big{animation:reelBig 1.6s ease-in-out infinite}
@keyframes reelBig{0%,100%{transform:scale(1)}50%{transform:scale(1.08)}}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){.reelPanel.shake,#reelTitle.big{animation:none}}
.cTier{font-size:19px;color:var(--lamp);margin:0}
.cEvt{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr auto;gap:2px 14px;align-items:center;text-align:left;padding:7px 11px;font:inherit;color:var(--paper);background:var(--plum);border:3px solid var(--ink);border-radius:0;cursor:pointer;box-shadow:inset -3px -3px 0 rgba(0,0,0,.35),inset 3px 3px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.1),3px 3px 0 var(--ink)}
.cEvt:hover:not([aria-disabled="true"]){background:var(--plum2);border-color:var(--lamp)}
.cEvt[aria-disabled="true"]{opacity:.55;cursor:default}
.cEvtName{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:10px;line-height:1.4}
.cEvtTxt{font-size:18px;opacity:.85}
.cEvtState{grid-row:1 / span 2;grid-column:2;font-size:19px;color:var(--lamp);text-align:right}
#cNote{grid-column:auto;margin:8px 0 0}#cNote:empty{display:none}
.cTools{display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-left:auto}.cTools .btn{font-size:8px;padding:7px 9px}
.stars{image-rendering:pixelated;flex:none}
.cInfo{margin:0 0 8px;font-size:19px}
.cEv{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,3fr) minmax(0,2fr);gap:14px;align-items:start}
.bracket{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,minmax(0,1fr));gap:5px;font-size:17px}
.bracket.bField{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr))}
.bCol{display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:space-around;gap:3px;min-height:100%}
.bName{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:4px;padding:1px 5px;background:rgba(13,10,28,.6);border-left:3px solid var(--plum2);white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden}
.bName span:first-child{overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis}
.bName.you{color:var(--lamp);border-left-color:var(--lamp)}.bName.out{opacity:.45}.bName.champ{border-left-color:var(--lamp);color:var(--lamp)}
.cOpp{display:grid;grid-template-columns:auto 1fr;gap:4px 10px;align-content:start;font-size:18px}
.cOpp .portrait{grid-row:1 / span 3}
.cOppName{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:9px;line-height:1.5;color:var(--lamp)}
.cOppTxt{grid-column:1 / -1;opacity:.9}
@media (max-width:640px){.cEv{grid-template-columns:1fr}}
#sc-setup .row{grid-template-columns:96px 1fr;margin-bottom:10px}#sc-setup .note{grid-column:2;font-size:18px;opacity:.72}
@media (max-width:760px),(max-height:500px){#lockTabs{flex-wrap:nowrap;flex:none}#menu #lockTabs .btn{padding-left:5px;padding-right:5px}#menu #lockTabs .ib{width:32px;height:auto;align-self:stretch}#lockTabs .ib svg{width:16px;height:16px}}   /* phones: the locker's tabs keep to one row */
@media (max-height:500px){.lockTop{flex-wrap:nowrap}.lockTop .cInfo{min-width:0;line-height:1}}   /* on its side, with the words beside them wrapping */
@media (max-width:500px){#sc-setup .row{grid-template-columns:1fr;gap:4px}#sc-setup .note{grid-column:1}   .caseGrid{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr))}}   /* narrow upright phones: labels above, so the buttons wrap less */
@media (max-width:560px) and (min-height:501px){#menu{padding-top:66px}#menuPanel{max-height:calc(97vh - 66px)}.lockList{max-height:calc(97vh - 304px)}}   /* upright phones: the panel starts below the corner buttons, so a tall one (the locker's lists) never runs under them */
/* short screens (a phone on its side): everything on one screen, no scrolling */
@media (max-height:500px){
  #menu{padding:2vh 3vw}
  .title{font-size:22px;margin-bottom:10px}
  #mStage{padding:10px 14px 8px}.mTop{padding:8px 14px 0}.mFoot{padding:6px 14px 8px}
  .cards{grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(200px,1fr));gap:7px}
  .card{padding:6px 9px;gap:9px}.cardArt{height:26px}.cardName{font-size:9px}.cardTxt{font-size:16px}.cardBody{gap:4px}
  #bStart,#bQuick,#bCreate,#bRejoin,#bCareer{font-size:10px;padding:9px 12px}.quickRow>.btn{margin-bottom:8px}
  #sc-cnew .row{grid-template-columns:78px 1fr;gap:3px 8px;margin-bottom:6px}#sc-cnew .lbl{font-size:18px;padding-top:3px}#sc-cnew .note{display:none}
  #cName{font-size:18px;padding:2px 6px}.cStyleBtn{padding:2px 4px}.cCols .swatch{width:20px;height:20px}
  .cTop{margin-bottom:6px}.cMeName{font-size:10px;margin-bottom:3px}.cMeStats{font-size:17px}.cEvents{gap:4px}
  .cEvt{grid-template-columns:auto 1fr auto;padding:4px 9px}.cEvtName{font-size:8px}.cEvtTxt,.cEvtState,.cTier{font-size:16px}
  .cEvtState{grid-row:1;grid-column:3}.cEvtTxt{grid-row:1;grid-column:2;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis}.cColLbl{font-size:16px}
  .cInfo{font-size:16px;margin-bottom:4px}.bracket{font-size:15px;gap:3px}.cOpp{font-size:16px}.cOppName{font-size:8px}
  .cTiers{margin-bottom:5px}.lockList{max-height:calc(94vh - 158px)}.invRow{padding:3px 7px;margin-bottom:4px}.invName{font-size:8px}.invSub,.colRw{font-size:15px}.colTile{width:48px;height:48px}.colTile.sale{height:56px}.invRow{grid-template-columns:auto auto 1fr auto;grid-template-rows:auto}.invRow>img,.invRow>.fxIco{grid-row:1}.invSub{grid-column:3;grid-row:1}.invState{grid-column:4}.fxRow .invSub{display:none}.fxRow .btn{grid-column:4;justify-self:end}.colHead{margin:8px 0 4px}
  .lockTop{margin-bottom:5px}.lockTop .cInfo{font-size:16px}.invRow>img.portrait{max-height:24px}.colTile>img.portrait{max-height:28px}.caseGrid{gap:5px}.caseCard{padding:4px 4px;gap:2px}
  .caseCard>img{width:36px;height:28px}.caseName{font-size:8px}.caseN{font-size:16px}.caseOdds{font-size:14px}.caseBtns .btn{padding:5px 5px}
  .lockFoot{margin-top:5px;font-size:15px}.lockFoot .btn{padding:4px 8px}#lockNote{margin-top:3px;font-size:16px}
  #reelTitle{margin-bottom:2vh}#reelTxt{margin:2vh auto 1vh;min-height:2.2em}#reelBtns .btn{font-size:10px;padding:9px 12px}
  #ver{margin-top:6px;font-size:15px}
  #sc-setup .row{grid-template-columns:78px 1fr;gap:3px 8px;margin-bottom:6px}#sc-setup .lbl{font-size:18px;padding-top:3px}
  #sc-setup .note{display:none}#menu .seg{gap:4px}#menu .seg .btn{font-size:8px;padding:6px 7px}
  #sc-online .onl{gap:5px}#roomList{max-height:78px}#netName,#netCode{font-size:18px;padding:2px 6px}
  .mFoot .btn{font-size:8px;padding:7px 9px}
  #menuIcons{top:calc(2vh + 7px);right:calc(3vw + 9px);gap:6px}.iconBtn{padding:5px}.iconBtn svg{width:16px;height:16px}
  #bMoney{padding:5px 8px;gap:5px;font-size:9px}#bMoney svg{width:16px;height:16px}.moneyDelta{font-size:8px}
  .ideaPanel{padding:10px 14px 10px}.ideaPanel h2{font-size:10px;margin-bottom:6px}.ideaPanel .actions{margin-top:6px}#ideasList{max-height:calc(92vh - 150px)}.ideaText{font-size:18px}#ideaPop{font-size:8px;margin-top:6px}
}


/* in-game hud */
#board{position:fixed;top:8px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:stretch;pointer-events:none}
.pl,#mid{padding:6px 10px;background:rgba(22,17,44,.86);border:3px solid var(--plum2);box-shadow:5px 5px 0 var(--ink)}
.pl{min-width:158px}
.pl.active{border-color:var(--lamp)}
.pname{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:8px;margin-bottom:5px;white-space:nowrap}
.pl.active .pname::before{content:'\25B6  ';color:var(--lamp)}
.pgroup{font-size:17px;opacity:.85}
.pballs,#rack{display:flex;gap:3px;margin-top:4px;min-height:14px}
#rack{justify-content:center}
#rack:empty{display:none}
.bi{width:12px;height:12px;border-radius:50%;border:2px solid var(--ink);display:inline-block}
.bi.gone{opacity:.2}
.bi.ring{outline:2px solid var(--lamp);outline-offset:1px}
#mid{text-align:center;min-width:150px}
#modeName{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:8px;color:var(--lamp)}
#turnLine{font-size:19px;margin-top:4px}
#extra{font-size:17px;color:var(--chalk);margin-top:2px}
#extra:empty{display:none}
#score{font-size:16px;margin-top:3px;opacity:.8}
#score:empty{display:none}
#notices{position:fixed;top:calc(var(--bb,112px) + 10px);left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:6px;pointer-events:none;width:max-content;max-width:94vw;z-index:5}
.toast{padding:5px 12px;font-size:20px;background:var(--ink);border:3px solid var(--lamp);opacity:0;transform:translateY(-6px);transition:opacity .2s,transform .2s;text-align:center;max-width:94vw}
.toast.show{opacity:1;transform:none}
.toast[data-kind=foul]{border-color:var(--rose);color:#ffd8e0}
.toast[data-kind=good]{border-color:var(--chalk)}
#thinking{font-size:19px;padding:5px 10px;background:rgba(13,10,28,.8);border:3px solid var(--plum2)}
#thinking::after{content:'...';display:inline-block;width:1.4em;text-align:left;animation:dots 1.2s steps(4) infinite;overflow:hidden;vertical-align:bottom}
@keyframes dots{0%{width:0}100%{width:1.4em}}
#tools{position:fixed;top:8px;right:8px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;align-items:flex-end}
#practice{position:fixed;top:8px;left:8px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;align-items:flex-start}
#shot{position:fixed;right:10px;bottom:10px;display:grid;grid-template-columns:auto auto;gap:8px 10px;padding:9px 10px;align-items:start;transition:opacity .2s}
#shot.dim{opacity:.5}
#shot.away,#tools.away,#practice.away{opacity:.12;transition-duration:.6s}
#tools,#practice{transition:opacity .2s}
#shot.noSpin{grid-template-columns:auto}#shot.noSpin .spinWrap{display:none}#shot.noSpin .powWrap{justify-self:center}
.spinWrap{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:6px}
.powWrap{display:grid;grid-template-columns:auto auto;gap:6px;align-items:start;justify-items:center}
#powTxt{grid-column:1 / -1}
#bSpin{padding:3px;line-height:0}#spinMini{width:24px;height:24px;image-rendering:pixelated}
#spin{width:96px;height:96px;image-rendering:pixelated;cursor:crosshair;touch-action:none}
#spinTxt{font-size:17px;line-height:1.05;height:2.1em;overflow:hidden;white-space:pre-line;text-align:center;width:110px}
#pow{position:relative;width:24px;height:122px;background:var(--ink);border:3px solid var(--plum2);cursor:ns-resize;touch-action:none}
#powFill{position:absolute;left:0;right:0;bottom:0;background:linear-gradient(to top,var(--chalk) 0 33%,var(--lamp) 33% 70%,var(--rose) 70% 100%);background-size:100% 116px;background-position:bottom}
#powTxt{font-size:17px;white-space:nowrap;min-width:64px;text-align:center;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;padding:1px 4px;cursor:ew-resize;touch-action:none;background:var(--ink) repeating-linear-gradient(90deg,rgba(255,255,255,.14) 0 1px,transparent 1px 6px);border:2px solid var(--plum2)}   /* slide along it to set the power finely, like the aim readout */
#aimTxt{flex:1;text-align:center;font-size:17px;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;padding:2px 4px;cursor:ew-resize;touch-action:none;background:var(--ink) repeating-linear-gradient(90deg,rgba(255,255,255,.14) 0 1px,transparent 1px 6px);border:2px solid var(--plum2)}   /* slide along it to turn finely; its marks move with the aim */
#bShoot{grid-column:1 / -1;font-size:10px;padding:10px;background:var(--lamp);color:var(--ink)}
#bShoot:hover{background:#ffd48e}
.ib svg{display:none}
#ibTip{position:fixed;z-index:9;padding:3px 8px;font-size:17px;background:var(--ink);border:2px solid var(--lamp);pointer-events:none;white-space:nowrap}
#help kbd.tap{cursor:pointer}#help kbd.tap:active{background:var(--lamp);color:var(--ink)}
.tapNote{color:var(--chalk)}
#bihTip{font-size:19px;text-align:center;padding:5px 10px;background:rgba(13,10,28,.85);border:3px solid #7dffb0}
#help{position:fixed;left:10px;bottom:10px;width:min(400px,92vw);max-height:80vh;overflow:auto;padding:12px 14px 10px;font-size:18px}
#help h2,#pause h2{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-weight:400;font-size:12px;color:var(--lamp);margin:0 0 10px}
#help dl{display:grid;grid-template-columns:auto 1fr;gap:6px 12px;margin:0 0 12px}
#help dt{color:var(--lamp)}
#help dd{margin:0}
#help p{margin:0 0 8px;max-width:48ch}
#bHelpClose{margin-top:4px}

/* overlays */
.overlay{position:fixed;inset:0;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;background:rgba(13,10,28,.35);padding:4vh 4vw}
/* Pause and Settings: tabs along the top (Match in a game, then Play, Looks, Display, Sound, and Dev in dev mode), each
   tab its own page that scrolls; a large panel on a computer (a click outside it closes it), the whole screen on a phone */
#pause,#help,#idea,#ideas{z-index:7}   /* above the game's messages and the menus' corner icons */
#pause{background:rgba(13,10,28,.6)}
#pausePanel{display:flex;flex-direction:column;width:min(900px,94vw);height:min(620px,92vh);padding:0;background:rgba(22,17,44,.97);overflow:hidden;animation:pOpen .18s ease-out}
@keyframes pOpen{from{opacity:0;transform:scale(.97)}}
#pHead{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;flex:none;padding:0 14px 0 8px;border-bottom:3px solid var(--plum)}
#pTabs{position:relative;display:flex;flex:1;min-width:0;align-self:stretch;overflow-x:auto;scrollbar-width:none}
#pTabs::-webkit-scrollbar{display:none}
.pTab{flex:none;font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:12px;line-height:1;color:var(--paper);opacity:.55;background:none;border:0;border-radius:0;padding:22px 18px 20px;cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap;transition:opacity .15s,color .15s}
.pTab:hover{opacity:.9}
.pTab[aria-selected="true"]{color:var(--lamp);opacity:1}
.pTab:focus-visible{outline:3px dashed var(--chalk);outline-offset:-7px}
#pInk{position:absolute;left:0;bottom:0;width:0;height:4px;background:var(--lamp);pointer-events:none;transition:transform .22s ease,width .22s ease}
#bResume{flex:none;font-size:11px;padding:10px 14px;background:var(--lamp);color:var(--ink)}
#bResume:hover,.pMain:hover{background:#ffd48e}
#pPages{position:relative;flex:1;min-height:0}
.pPage{position:absolute;inset:0;overflow-y:auto;overscroll-behavior:contain;padding:12px 32px 24px}
.pPage.slide{animation:pSlide .22s ease-out}
@keyframes pSlide{from{opacity:0;transform:translateX(var(--dx,28px))}}
#pause .row{grid-template-columns:190px 1fr;gap:6px 18px;align-items:start;margin:0;padding:15px 0;border-bottom:2px solid rgba(78,50,112,.5)}
#pause .row:last-child{border-bottom:0}
#pause .lbl{font-size:25px;padding-top:6px}#pause .note{grid-column:2;font-size:18px;opacity:.6;max-width:58ch}
#pause .btn{font-size:11px;padding:10px 12px}#pause .seg{gap:7px}#pause .pickCur{font-size:22px}
#pgMatch:not([hidden]){display:flex;flex-direction:column}   /* the title and buttons centred top to bottom (auto margins, so a short screen scrolls rather than clips) */
#pause #pTitle{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-weight:400;font-size:18px;color:var(--lamp);text-align:center;margin:auto 0 8px}
#pSub{font-size:22px;text-align:center;opacity:.7;margin:0 0 24px}#pSub:empty{display:none}
#pauseGame{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:12px;width:min(380px,100%);margin:0 auto auto}
#pause .pBig{font-size:13px;padding:16px 18px}
#pause .pMain{background:var(--lamp);color:var(--ink)}
#pause .pQuiet{background:none;border-color:var(--plum2);box-shadow:none}#pause .pQuiet:hover{background:var(--plum)}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){#pausePanel,.pPage.slide{animation:none}#pInk{transition:none}}
@media (max-width:560px){#pause #pausePanel .row{grid-template-columns:1fr}#pause #pausePanel .note{grid-column:1}#pause .pTab{font-size:9px;padding:17px 8px 16px}#pHead{gap:6px}}   /* upright phones: the name above its buttons, and the tabs closer */
#overPanel{width:min(460px,92vw);padding:24px 24px 20px;text-align:left}
#overTitle{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-weight:400;font-size:26px;color:var(--lamp);margin:0 0 14px;text-shadow:3px 3px 0 var(--rose),6px 6px 0 var(--ink)}
#overWhy{font-size:24px;margin:0 0 6px}
#overStats{font-size:20px;opacity:.8;margin:0}
#overEarn{font-size:20px;color:var(--lamp);margin:6px 0 0}#overEarn:empty{display:none}

/* phones: narrow screens, and short ones such as a phone on its side */
@media (max-width:760px),(max-height:500px){
  html,body{font-size:19px}
  .row{grid-template-columns:1fr}.note{grid-column:1}
  #pause{padding:0}#pausePanel{width:100vw;height:100vh;height:100dvh;border:0;box-shadow:none}
  #pHead{padding:0 10px 0 4px}.pTab{font-size:11px;padding:17px 13px 16px}#bResume{font-size:10px;padding:9px 11px}
  .pPage{padding:4px 18px 18px}#pause .row{grid-template-columns:150px 1fr;padding:10px 0}#pause .note{grid-column:2}
  #pause .lbl{font-size:21px;padding-top:4px}#pause .note{font-size:17px}#pause .btn{font-size:11px;padding:11px 13px;min-height:42px}
  #pause #pTitle{font-size:14px;margin:auto 0 6px}#pSub{font-size:19px;margin-bottom:14px}#pauseGame{gap:9px}#pause .pBig{font-size:12px;padding:12px 16px}
  #board{top:6px;gap:5px;transform:translateX(-50%) scale(.82);transform-origin:top center}
  .pl{min-width:130px}
  #shot{right:6px;bottom:6px;padding:7px;gap:5px 6px}
  #spin{width:64px;height:64px}#spinTxt{width:84px;font-size:15px}#powTxt{min-width:56px;font-size:15px}#aimTxt{font-size:15px}
  #bSpin{padding:2px}#spinMini{width:22px;height:22px}
  #pow{height:92px}#powFill{background-size:100% 86px}
  .toast{font-size:18px}#thinking,#bihTip{font-size:17px}
  #tools{top:auto;bottom:8px;right:auto;left:8px;display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;max-width:44vw}
  #tools .btn,#practice .btn{font-size:8px;padding:7px 6px}
  #practice{top:auto;bottom:calc(var(--tb,120px) + 6px);display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;align-items:start;max-width:44vw}
  .ib{width:34px;height:34px;padding:0 !important;display:inline-grid;place-items:center;-webkit-touch-callout:none;-webkit-user-select:none;user-select:none}
  .ib svg{display:block;width:20px;height:20px}.ib .lab{display:none}
  #tools,#practice{grid-template-columns:repeat(4,34px);gap:5px;max-width:none}
  #aimRow .ib,#trickInfo .ib{width:30px;height:30px}#aimRow .ib svg,#trickInfo .ib svg{width:14px;height:14px}
  #replayBar .ib{width:32px;height:32px}
  #help{bottom:auto;top:8px;max-height:70vh}
}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){.toast,#shot,.card{transition:none}.card:hover{transform:none}#thinking::after{animation:none;width:1.4em}}
#hud #tools .btn,#hud #practice .btn{font-size:8px;padding:7px 9px}
input,textarea{font-family:'VT323',monospace;font-size:21px;color:var(--paper);background:var(--ink);border:3px solid var(--plum2);border-radius:0;padding:5px 8px;-webkit-user-select:text;user-select:text;outline:none}
input:focus,textarea:focus{border-color:var(--chalk)}
.onl{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px}
.onl .note{grid-column:auto}
.field{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;font-size:20px}
#netName{width:170px}#netCode{width:140px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:2px}
.note.warn{color:var(--rose)}.note.warn:empty{display:none}
#lobbyPanel{width:min(460px,92vw);padding:20px 22px}
#lobbyPanel h2{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-weight:400;font-size:12px;color:var(--lamp);margin:0 0 14px}
#lobbyCode{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:30px;letter-spacing:8px;color:var(--lamp);margin:6px 0 12px;text-shadow:3px 3px 0 var(--rose),5px 5px 0 var(--ink)}
#lobbyStatus{font-size:22px;margin:0 0 10px}
#lobbyLink{width:100%;font-size:17px}
#lobbyPanel .note{grid-column:auto;margin:12px 0 0}
#net{font-size:16px;margin-top:3px}#net:empty{display:none}
#net::before{content:'\25CF  ';color:#7dffb0}#net[data-ok="0"]::before{color:var(--rose)}
/* open rooms */
.roomsHead{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:baseline;font-size:20px;color:var(--lamp);margin-top:4px}
#roomsLive{font-size:17px;color:var(--chalk)}
#roomList{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;max-height:172px;overflow:auto;padding:6px;background:rgba(13,10,28,.55);border:3px solid var(--plum2)}
#roomList .note{margin:2px 4px}
.roomItem{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;padding:5px 8px;background:var(--plum);border:3px solid var(--ink)}
.roomItem .rn{font-size:20px}.roomItem .rm{font-size:17px;opacity:.8}
.roomItem .btn{flex:none}
#chatPop{position:fixed;top:8px;right:118px;display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:6px;padding:8px;z-index:6}
.toast[data-kind=chat]{border-color:#7dffb0}
#aimRow{grid-column:1 / -1;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:6px}
#aimRow .btn{padding:8px 12px;font-size:10px;touch-action:none}
#trickInfo{padding:8px 10px;max-width:250px;font-size:17px}
#trickTitle{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:8px;line-height:1.5;color:var(--lamp);margin-bottom:4px}
#trickGoal{margin-bottom:6px}
#trickInfo .btn{font-size:8px;padding:7px 8px}
#replayBar{position:fixed;left:50%;bottom:12px;transform:translateX(-50%);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:7px 10px;font-size:19px;z-index:6}
#ballPick{position:fixed;left:50%;bottom:10px;transform:translateX(-50%);width:min(300px,60vw);padding:10px 12px 12px;z-index:6}
#ballPick p{margin:0 0 8px;font-size:18px;line-height:1.1}
#ballPick .actions{margin-top:10px}
#ballGrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(8,1fr);gap:4px}
.bp{padding:3px;background:var(--ink);border:2px solid var(--plum2);cursor:pointer;line-height:0;opacity:.45}
.bp canvas{width:100%;height:auto;image-rendering:pixelated}
.bp[aria-pressed="true"]{opacity:1;border-color:var(--lamp)}
@media (max-width:760px) and (orientation:portrait){#ballPick{bottom:auto;top:calc(var(--bb,90px) + 10px);width:min(300px,90vw)}}   /* upright phones: no room at the bottom */
@media (max-height:500px){#ballPick{bottom:6px;padding:6px 8px 8px;left:calc(var(--lw,40vw) + 8px);right:calc(var(--sw,180px) + 8px);width:auto;transform:none}#ballGrid{grid-template-columns:repeat(5,1fr)}#ballPick p{font-size:15px;margin-bottom:5px}#ballPick .actions{margin-top:6px}#ballPick .btn{font-size:8px;padding:6px 8px}}
#rotate{position:fixed;left:50%;top:50%;transform:translate(-50%,-50%);width:min(300px,86vw);padding:14px 16px 16px;text-align:center;z-index:8}
#rotate p{margin:0 0 12px;font-size:22px}
.offerPanel{width:min(420px,92vw);padding:18px 20px}.offerPanel p{margin:0 0 6px;font-size:22px}
@media (max-width:760px),(max-height:500px){#chatPop{top:auto;bottom:calc(var(--tb,120px) + 6px);right:auto;left:8px}#replayBar{bottom:auto;top:calc(var(--bb,90px) + 10px)}#trickInfo{grid-column:1 / -1;max-width:none;width:min(300px,42vw)}#aimRow .btn{padding:7px 9px}}
</style>
</head>
<body>
<canvas id="view" aria-label="3D pool table"></canvas>
<div id="scan"></div>

<div id="hud" hidden>
  <div id="board">
    <div class="pl" id="p0"><div class="pname"></div><div class="pgroup"></div><div class="pballs"></div></div>
    <div id="mid"><div id="modeName"></div><div id="turnLine"></div><div id="extra"></div><div id="net"></div><div id="rack"></div><div id="score"></div></div>
    <div class="pl" id="p1"><div class="pname"></div><div class="pgroup"></div><div class="pballs"></div></div>
  </div>
  <div id="notices" role="status" aria-live="polite">
    <div id="thinking" hidden>CPU is lining up a shot</div>
    <div id="bihTip" hidden>Ball in hand: drag the cue ball to place it</div>
  </div>
  <div id="tools">
    <button class="btn" id="bCam" aria-pressed="false" title="Cue view (C)">Cue view</button>
    <button class="btn" id="bTop" aria-pressed="false" title="Overhead view (T)">Overhead</button>
    <button class="btn" id="bHelp" title="Controls (H)">Controls</button>
    <button class="btn" id="bPause" title="Pause (Esc)">Pause</button>
    <button class="btn" id="bFull" aria-pressed="false" hidden>Fullscreen</button>
    <button class="btn" id="bReplay" title="Replay the last shot (V)" hidden>Replay</button>
    <button class="btn" id="bChat" title="Quick chat" hidden>Chat</button>
  </div>
  <div id="chatPop" class="panel" hidden></div>
  <div id="practice" hidden>
    <button class="btn" id="bRerack" title="R">Rerack</button>
    <button class="btn" id="bUndo" title="U">Undo shot</button>
    <button class="btn" id="bHand" aria-pressed="false" title="B">Ball in hand</button>
    <button class="btn" id="bMove" aria-pressed="false">Edit table</button>
    <button class="btn" id="bBalls" aria-pressed="false" hidden>Add balls</button>
    <button class="btn" id="bSave">Save layout</button>
    <div id="trickInfo" class="panel" hidden>
      <div id="trickTitle"></div><div id="trickGoal"></div>
      <div class="seg"><button class="btn" id="bTrickPrev" aria-label="Previous trick">&lt;</button><button class="btn" id="bTrickNext" aria-label="Next trick">&gt;</button><button class="btn" id="bShowMe">Show me</button><button class="btn" id="bDelLayout">Delete</button></div>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div id="shot" class="panel">
    <div class="spinWrap"><canvas id="spin" width="32" height="32" aria-label="Cue tip position: drag to add spin"></canvas><div id="spinTxt">Centre ball</div></div>
    <div class="powWrap"><button class="btn" id="bSpin" aria-pressed="true" title="Show or hide the spin diagram"><canvas id="spinMini" width="16" height="16"></canvas><span class="vh">Spin diagram</span></button><div id="pow" aria-label="Power: drag to set"><div id="powFill"></div></div><div id="powTxt" title="Slide along here to set the power very finely">0.00 m/s</div></div>
    <div id="aimRow"><button class="btn" id="bAimL" aria-label="Turn aim left">&lt;</button><div id="aimTxt" title="Slide along here to turn very finely">Aim 0.00°</div><button class="btn" id="bAimR" aria-label="Turn aim right">&gt;</button></div>
    <button class="btn" id="bShoot">Shoot</button>
  </div>
  <div id="ibTip" hidden></div>
  <div id="help" class="panel" hidden>
    <h2>Controls</h2>
    <p class="tapNote">Tap a key to use it.</p>
    <dl>
      <dt>Aim</dt><dd>Drag across the table and the cue points at your cursor. Double-click to aim at a spot.</dd>
      <dt>Fine aim</dt><dd><kbd>←</kbd><kbd>→</kbd> or <kbd>A</kbd><kbd>D</kbd> turn 0.05° per press (holding does nothing more); <span data-ctrl>with <kbd>Ctrl</kbd>, <kbd>←</kbd><kbd>→</kbd></span> turn 0.01°. Hold <kbd>Shift</kbd> with them to turn freely. On a touch screen, slide along the aim readout to turn very finely.</dd>
      <dt>Power</dt><dd>Hold <kbd>↑</kbd><kbd>↓</kbd> or <kbd>W</kbd><kbd>S</kbd>, or drag the bar. Add <kbd>Shift</kbd> to go slowly.</dd>
      <dt>Fine power</dt><dd><span data-ctrl><kbd>Ctrl</kbd> + <kbd>↑</kbd><kbd>↓</kbd></span> changes the speed by 0.01 m/s per press (holding does nothing more). On a touch screen, slide along the speed readout under the power bar.</dd>
      <dt>Spin</dt><dd>Drag on the cue ball, or <kbd>I</kbd><kbd>J</kbd><kbd>K</kbd><kbd>L</kbd>. <kbd>O</kbd> resets. The small ball by the power bar shows or hides the diagram.</dd>
      <dt>Shoot</dt><dd><kbd>Space</kbd> or the Shoot button.</dd>
      <dt>Camera</dt><dd>Right-drag (or <kbd>Alt</kbd>-drag) to orbit, wheel to zoom, middle-drag to pan, <kbd>Q</kbd><kbd>E</kbd> to turn.</dd>
      <dt>Views</dt><dd><kbd>C</kbd> cue view, <kbd>T</kbd> overhead, <kbd>F</kbd> reset.</dd>
      <dt>Touch</dt><dd>One finger aims. Two fingers orbit and pinch to zoom.</dd>
      <dt>Replay</dt><dd><kbd>V</kbd> or the Replay button watches the last shot again, with slow motion. Orbit the camera while it plays.</dd>
```
