      <dt>Practice</dt><dd><kbd>R</kbd> rerack, <kbd>U</kbd> undo, <kbd>B</kbd> ball in hand. Edit table lets you drag any ball and add or remove balls; Save layout keeps it under Trick shots.</dd>
    </dl>
    <p>8-ball: pot your group, then the 8. A scratch, hitting the wrong ball first, or no ball reaching a rail after contact is a foul and gives your opponent ball in hand.</p>
    <p>9-ball: always hit the lowest ball first. Pot the 9 on a legal shot to win.</p>
    <p>Reds &amp; yellows (British pub rules): pot your colour, then the black. Fouls are an in-off, missing every ball, hitting the wrong colour or the black first, or potting your opponent's colour. After a foul your opponent gets two visits, and the first is a free ball: they may hit any ball first. An in-off also gives ball in hand behind the baulk line. Potting the black early, or with a foul, loses the frame.</p>
    <p>Side spin makes the cue ball squirt slightly off line. The Full and Ghost + line guides allow for it; the others don't.</p>
    <button class="btn" id="bHelpClose">Close</button>
  </div>
</div>

<div id="menu">
  <div id="menuPanel" class="panel">
    <div class="mTop" id="mTop" hidden><button class="btn" id="mBack" type="button" aria-label="Back">&lt; Back</button><h2 id="mTitle"></h2></div>
    <div id="mStage">
      <section class="mScreen" id="sc-home">
        <h1 class="title">Retro Rack</h1>
        <div class="quickRow"><button class="btn" id="bRejoin" type="button" hidden></button><button class="btn" id="bQuick" type="button" hidden></button></div>
        <div class="cards">
          <button class="card" type="button" data-go="single" data-art="single"><span class="cardName">Single player</span><span class="cardTxt">Computer or practice</span></button>
          <button class="card" type="button" data-go="multi" data-art="multi"><span class="cardName">Multiplayer</span><span class="cardTxt">Online or same device</span></button>
          <button class="card" type="button" data-go="locker" data-art="locker"><span class="cardName">Locker</span><span class="cardTxt" id="lockerTxt">Cases and looks</span></button>
        </div>
        <p id="ver"></p>
      </section>
      <section class="mScreen" id="sc-single" data-title="Single player" hidden>
        <div class="cards">
          <button class="card" type="button" data-go="game" data-opp="bot" data-art="cpu"><span class="cardName">Vs computer</span><span class="cardTxt">Four skill levels</span></button>
          <button class="card" type="button" data-go="practice" data-art="practice"><span class="cardName">Practice</span><span class="cardTxt">Free play, trick shots</span></button>
          <button class="card" type="button" data-go="career" data-art="career"><span class="cardName">Career</span><span class="cardTxt">From the pub to the top</span></button>
        </div>
      </section>
      <section class="mScreen" id="sc-cnew" data-title="New career" hidden>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Name</div><div class="seg"><input id="cName" maxlength="14" placeholder="Your name" autocomplete="nickname"></div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Look</div><div class="seg" id="cStyle"></div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Colours</div><div class="seg cCols"><span class="cColLbl">Skin</span><span class="swatches" id="cSkin"></span><span class="cColLbl">Hair</span><span class="swatches" id="cHair"></span><span class="cColLbl">Shirt</span><span class="swatches" id="cShirt"></span></div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Aim guide</div><div class="seg" id="cGuide"></div><div class="note" id="cGuideTxt"></div></div>
      </section>
      <section class="mScreen" id="sc-career" data-title="Career" hidden>
        <div class="cTop"><div class="cMe" id="cMe"></div><div class="cTools"><button class="btn" id="cShop" type="button">Locker</button><button class="btn" id="cExport" type="button">Save to file</button><button class="btn" id="cImport" type="button">Load from file</button><button class="btn" id="cRetire" type="button">Retire</button><input type="file" id="cFile" accept=".json,application/json" hidden></div></div>
        <div class="seg cTiers" id="cTiers"></div>
        <div class="cEvents" id="cEvents"></div>
        <div class="note" id="cNote" role="status"></div>
      </section>
      <section class="mScreen" id="sc-locker" data-title="Locker" hidden>
        <div class="lockTop"><p class="cInfo" id="lockMoney"></p><div class="seg" id="lockTabs"></div></div>
        <div id="lockBody"></div>
        <div class="lockFoot" id="lockPages"></div>
        <div class="note" id="lockNote" role="status"></div>
      </section>
      <section class="mScreen" id="sc-cevent" hidden>
        <p class="cInfo" id="cInfo"></p>
        <div class="cEv"><div class="bracket" id="cBracket"></div><div class="cOpp" id="cOpp"></div></div>
      </section>
      <section class="mScreen" id="sc-multi" data-title="Multiplayer" hidden>
        <div class="cards">
          <button class="card" type="button" data-go="online" data-opp="online" data-art="online"><span class="cardName">Online</span><span class="cardTxt">Rooms and invite links</span></button>
          <button class="card" type="button" data-go="game" data-opp="friend" data-art="local"><span class="cardName">Same device</span><span class="cardTxt">Take turns</span></button>
        </div>
      </section>
      <section class="mScreen" id="sc-game" data-title="Choose a game" hidden>
        <div class="cards">
          <button class="card" type="button" data-mode="8ball" data-art="8ball"><span class="cardName">8-ball</span><span class="cardTxt"></span></button>
          <button class="card" type="button" data-mode="9ball" data-art="9ball"><span class="cardName">9-ball</span><span class="cardTxt"></span></button>
          <button class="card" type="button" data-mode="uk8" data-art="uk8"><span class="cardName">Reds &amp; yellows</span><span class="cardTxt"></span></button>
        </div>
      </section>
      <section class="mScreen" id="sc-setup" hidden>
        <div class="row" id="rowDiff"><div class="lbl">Skill</div><div class="seg" id="mDiff"></div><div class="note" id="diffTxt"></div></div>
        <div class="row" id="rowRoom"><div class="lbl">Room</div><div class="seg" id="mListed"></div><div class="note" id="listedTxt"></div></div>
        <div class="row" id="rowBlack"><div class="lbl">On black</div><div class="seg" id="mBlack" title="Visits after a foul for a player on the black"></div></div>
        <div class="row" id="rowMatch"><div class="lbl">Match</div><div class="seg" id="mRace"></div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Aim guide</div><div class="seg" id="mGuide"></div><div class="note" id="guideTxt"></div></div>
      </section>
      <section class="mScreen" id="sc-practice" data-title="Practice" hidden>
        <div class="cards">
          <button class="card" type="button" data-rack="8ball" data-art="r8"><span class="cardName">8-ball rack</span><span class="cardTxt">9 ft table</span></button>
          <button class="card" type="button" data-rack="9ball" data-art="r9"><span class="cardName">9-ball rack</span><span class="cardTxt">Diamond, 1 to 9</span></button>
          <button class="card" type="button" data-rack="uk" data-art="ruk"><span class="cardName">Reds &amp; yellows</span><span class="cardTxt">7 ft table</span></button>
          <button class="card" type="button" data-rack="scatter" data-art="scatter"><span class="cardName">Scatter</span><span class="cardTxt">Balls spread out</span></button>
          <button class="card" type="button" data-rack="trick" data-art="trick"><span class="cardName">Trick shots</span><span class="cardTxt">With demos</span></button>
        </div>
      </section>
      <section class="mScreen" id="sc-online" data-title="Online" hidden>
        <div class="onl">
          <label class="field">Your name <input id="netName" maxlength="14" placeholder="Player" autocomplete="nickname"></label>
          <div class="roomsHead"><span>Open rooms</span><span id="roomsLive"></span></div>
          <div id="roomList"></div>
          <div class="seg"><input id="netCode" maxlength="8" placeholder="Room code" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false" aria-label="Room code"><button class="btn" id="bJoin" type="button">Join with code</button></div>
          <div class="note warn" id="netNote" role="status"></div>
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
    <div class="mFoot"><button class="btn" id="bStart" type="button" hidden>Rack 'em up</button><button class="btn" id="bCreate" type="button" hidden>Create a room</button><button class="btn" id="bCareer" type="button" hidden></button><button class="btn" id="bWithdraw" type="button" hidden>Withdraw</button></div>
  </div>
</div>

<div id="pause" class="overlay" hidden>
  <div id="pausePanel" class="panel" role="dialog" aria-label="Settings">
    <div id="pHead">
      <div id="pTabs" role="tablist" aria-label="Settings"><button class="pTab" type="button" role="tab" id="tabMatch" data-tab="match" aria-controls="pgMatch" aria-selected="false" tabindex="-1">Match</button><button class="pTab" type="button" role="tab" id="tabPlay" data-tab="play" aria-controls="pgPlay" aria-selected="false" tabindex="-1">Play</button><button class="pTab" type="button" role="tab" id="tabLooks" data-tab="looks" aria-controls="pgLooks" aria-selected="false" tabindex="-1">Looks</button><button class="pTab" type="button" role="tab" id="tabDisplay" data-tab="display" aria-controls="pgDisplay" aria-selected="false" tabindex="-1">Display</button><button class="pTab" type="button" role="tab" id="tabSound" data-tab="sound" aria-controls="pgSound" aria-selected="false" tabindex="-1">Sound</button><button class="pTab" type="button" role="tab" id="tabDev" data-tab="dev" aria-controls="pgDev" aria-selected="false" tabindex="-1">Dev</button><span id="pInk" aria-hidden="true"></span></div>
      <button class="btn" id="bResume" type="button">Resume</button>
    </div>
    <div id="pPages">
      <section class="pPage" id="pgMatch" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="tabMatch" data-tab="match" hidden>
        <h2 id="pTitle">Paused</h2><p id="pSub"></p>
        <div id="pauseGame"><button class="btn pBig pMain" id="bResumeBig" type="button">Resume</button><button class="btn pBig" id="bRestart" type="button">Re-rack</button><button class="btn pBig" id="bOfferRerack" type="button">Offer re-rack</button><button class="btn pBig" id="bConcede" type="button">Concede frame</button><button class="btn pBig pQuiet" id="bQuit" type="button">Quit to menu</button></div>
      </section>
      <section class="pPage" id="pgPlay" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="tabPlay" data-tab="play" hidden>
        <div class="row" id="rowSGuide"><div class="lbl">Aim guide</div><div class="seg" id="sGuide"></div><div class="note" id="sGuideTxt"></div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Markers</div><div class="seg"><button class="btn" id="sMarkers"></button></div><div class="note">A pulsing gold halo round the balls you're on, once you have a colour. In 9-ball, the lowest ball.</div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Shot camera</div><div class="seg"><button class="btn" id="sShotCam"></button></div><div class="note">When you shoot, the view swings down to your eyes behind the cue, then back once the balls stop.</div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Follow next shot</div><div class="seg"><button class="btn" id="sFollow"></button></div><div class="note">When your turn comes round, the free view glides behind the cue ball to show it and the ball it points at. Cue view always does.</div></div>
      </section>

        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Colours</div><div class="seg" id="sLevels"></div><div class="note">Shades per colour channel. Lower is more retro.</div></div>

        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Outlines</div><div class="seg"><button class="btn" id="sOutline"></button></div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Scanlines</div><div class="seg"><button class="btn" id="sScan"></button></div></div>
      </section>
      <section class="pPage" id="pgSound" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="tabSound" data-tab="sound" hidden>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Sound</div><div class="seg" id="sVolume"></div></div>
        <div class="row" id="rowVibrate"><div class="lbl">Vibration</div><div class="seg"><button class="btn" id="sVibrate"></button></div><div class="note">A short buzz when you pot a ball, a longer one on a foul.</div></div>
      </section>
      <section class="pPage" id="pgDev" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="tabDev" data-tab="dev" hidden>
        <div class="row" id="rowDev"><div class="lbl">Dev mode</div><div class="seg"><button class="btn" id="sDev" type="button">Switch off</button></div><div class="note">A test locker with every look; your own locker comes back when dev mode is off. The lightbulb in the menus' corner shows players' ideas.</div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Next case</div><div class="seg"><button class="btn" id="sDevMythic" type="button" aria-pressed="false">Next case mythic: Off</button></div><div class="note">The next case you open gives a mythic, then this switches itself off.</div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Test locker</div><div class="seg"><button class="btn" id="sDevEmpty" type="button">Empty test locker</button></div><div class="note">Empty starts it again with no looks and no rewards, so cases behave as they do for a new player (Treat cases always new, mythics never repeated). Fill gives it everything again.</div></div>
      </section>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

## relay/src/index.js

The Cloudflare Worker relay: a Room Durable Object per room code, and one Lobby for the open-rooms list. (241 lines)

```js
// Retro Rack relay: a tiny Cloudflare Worker that passes messages between the two players in a room,
// keeps a live list of open rooms, and holds the ideas players send in. It never runs the game itself;
// each browser checks every shot.
import { DurableObject } from "cloudflare:workers";

const MODES = ["8ball", "9ball", "uk8"];
const cleanName = s => String(s || "").replace(/[\u0000-\u001f<>]/g, "").trim().slice(0, 14) || "Player";
const lobbyStub = env => env.LOBBY.get(env.LOBBY.idFromName("lobby"));
const ideasStub = env => env.IDEAS.get(env.IDEAS.idFromName("ideas"));
// ideas are posted as plain text (a JSON body), so browsers send them straight from the game's page with no preflight
const CORS = { "access-control-allow-origin": "*", "access-control-allow-methods": "POST, OPTIONS", "access-control-allow-headers": "content-type" };

export default {
  async fetch(request, env) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    const isWs = request.headers.get("Upgrade") === "websocket";
    if (url.pathname === "/ideas") {
      if (request.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response(null, { status: 204, headers: CORS });
      if (request.method !== "POST") return new Response("Send ideas from the game.", { status: 405, headers: CORS });
      return ideasStub(env).fetch(request);
    }
    if (url.pathname === "/lobby") {
      if (!isWs) return new Response("This address expects a WebSocket connection.", { status: 426 });
      return lobbyStub(env).fetch(request);
    }
    const m = url.pathname.match(/^\/room\/([A-Za-z0-9]{3,12})$/);
    if (!m) {
      return new Response("Retro Rack relay is running.", { headers: { "content-type": "text/plain; charset=utf-8" } });
    }
    if (!isWs) return new Response("This address expects a WebSocket connection.", { status: 426 });
    return env.ROOMS.get(env.ROOMS.idFromName(m[1].toUpperCase())).fetch(request);
  },
};

// One Durable Object per room. Uses the WebSocket Hibernation API, so an idle room costs nothing.
export class Room extends DurableObject {
  constructor(ctx, env) {
    super(ctx, env);
    this.ctx.setWebSocketAutoResponse(new WebSocketRequestResponsePair("ping", "pong"));
  }

  live(except) {
    return this.ctx.getWebSockets()
      .filter(ws => ws !== except)
      .map(ws => ({ ws, a: ws.deserializeAttachment() || {} }))
      .filter(s => s.a.seat === 0 || s.a.seat === 1);
  }

  async lobby(op, arg) {
    try { await lobbyStub(this.env)[op](arg); } catch (e) { /* the room still works if the list is unavailable */ }
  }

  async fetch(request) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    const code = (url.pathname.split("/").pop() || "").toUpperCase();
    const cid = (url.searchParams.get("cid") || "").replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9]/g, "").slice(0, 32) || crypto.randomUUID();
    const { 0: client, 1: server } = new WebSocketPair();
    this.ctx.acceptWebSocket(server);

    let others = this.live(server);
    // the same browser tab reconnecting replaces its old connection and keeps its seat
    let seat = -1;
    for (const s of others) if (s.a.cid === cid) { seat = s.a.seat; s.ws.serializeAttachment({ cid, seat: -1 }); try { s.ws.close(4000, "replaced"); } catch (e) {} }
    others = others.filter(s => s.a.cid !== cid);
    const taken = new Set(others.map(s => s.a.seat));
    const seats = (await this.ctx.storage.get("seats")) || {};
    if (seat < 0) for (const k of [0, 1]) if (seats[k] === cid && !taken.has(k)) seat = k;
    if (seat < 0) for (const k of [0, 1]) if (!taken.has(k)) { seat = k; break; }

    if (seat < 0) {
      server.serializeAttachment({ cid, seat: -1 });
      server.send(JSON.stringify({ t: "full" }));
      server.close(4001, "room full");
      return new Response(null, { status: 101, webSocket: client });
    }
    server.serializeAttachment({ cid, seat });
    seats[seat] = cid;
    await this.ctx.storage.put("seats", seats);
    await this.ctx.storage.deleteAlarm();

    // open-rooms list: a listed room shows while its creator waits, and disappears once a second player joins
    const meta = (await this.ctx.storage.get("meta")) || { code };
    if (others.length > 0) {
      if (!meta.played) { meta.played = true; await this.ctx.storage.put("meta", meta); if (meta.listed) await this.lobby("removeRoom", code); }
    } else if (url.searchParams.get("list") === "1" && !meta.played) {
      const mode = MODES.includes(url.searchParams.get("mode")) ? url.searchParams.get("mode") : "8ball";
      Object.assign(meta, { listed: true, name: cleanName(url.searchParams.get("name")), mode });
      await this.ctx.storage.put("meta", meta);
      await this.lobby("addRoom", { code, name: meta.name, mode });
    }

    server.send(JSON.stringify({ t: "welcome", seat, peer: others.length > 0 }));
    for (const s of others) { try { s.ws.send(JSON.stringify({ t: "peer", on: true })); } catch (e) {} }
    return new Response(null, { status: 101, webSocket: client });
  }

  async webSocketMessage(ws, message) {
    if (typeof message !== "string" || message.length > 20000) return;
    const a = ws.deserializeAttachment() || {};
    if (a.seat !== 0 && a.seat !== 1) return;
    let o;
    try { o = JSON.parse(message); } catch (e) { return; }
    if (!o || typeof o.t !== "string") return;
    o.from = a.seat;
    const out = JSON.stringify(o);
    for (const s of this.live(ws)) if (s.a.seat !== a.seat) { try { s.ws.send(out); } catch (e) {} }
  }

  async webSocketClose(ws, code) {
    await this.gone(ws);
    try { ws.close(code === 1005 || code === 1006 ? 1000 : code, "closing"); } catch (e) {}
  }

  async webSocketError(ws) { await this.gone(ws); }

  async gone(ws) {
    const a = ws.deserializeAttachment() || {};
    const rest = this.live(ws);
    if ((a.seat === 0 || a.seat === 1) && !rest.some(s => s.a.seat === a.seat)) {
      for (const s of rest) { try { s.ws.send(JSON.stringify({ t: "peer", on: false })); } catch (e) {} }
    }
    if (!rest.length) {
      const meta = (await this.ctx.storage.get("meta")) || {};
      if (meta.listed && !meta.played) await this.lobby("removeRoom", meta.code);
      await this.ctx.storage.setAlarm(Date.now() + 6 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
    }
  }

  // tidy up rooms nobody has used for six hours
  async alarm() {
    if (!this.ctx.getWebSockets().length) await this.ctx.storage.deleteAll();
  }
}

// A single Durable Object holding the list of open rooms. Menus watch it over a WebSocket and get
// pushed a fresh list whenever a room opens or closes.
export class Lobby extends DurableObject {
  constructor(ctx, env) {
    super(ctx, env);
    this.ctx.setWebSocketAutoResponse(new WebSocketRequestResponsePair("ping", "pong"));
  }

  async rooms() {
    const r = (await this.ctx.storage.get("rooms")) || {};
    const cutoff = Date.now() - 45 * 60 * 1000;          // forget rooms left waiting for 45 minutes
    for (const k of Object.keys(r)) if (r[k].at < cutoff) delete r[k];
    return r;
  }

  view(r) { return Object.values(r).sort((a, b) => b.at - a.at).slice(0, 30); }

  push(r, only) {
    const msg = JSON.stringify({ t: "rooms", rooms: this.view(r), now: Date.now() });
    for (const ws of only ? [only] : this.ctx.getWebSockets()) { try { ws.send(msg); } catch (e) {} }
  }

  async addRoom(e) {
    const r = await this.rooms();
    r[e.code] = { code: e.code, name: cleanName(e.name), mode: MODES.includes(e.mode) ? e.mode : "8ball", at: Date.now() };
    const keys = Object.keys(r).sort((a, b) => r[b].at - r[a].at);
    for (const k of keys.slice(50)) delete r[k];
    await this.ctx.storage.put("rooms", r);
    this.push(r);
  }

  async removeRoom(code) {
    const r = await this.rooms();
    if (!r[code]) return;
    delete r[code];
    await this.ctx.storage.put("rooms", r);
    this.push(r);
  }

  async fetch(request) {
    const { 0: client, 1: server } = new WebSocketPair();
    this.ctx.acceptWebSocket(server);
    this.push(await this.rooms(), server);
    return new Response(null, { status: 101, webSocket: client });
  }

  async webSocketMessage(ws, message) {
    if (message === "list") this.push(await this.rooms(), ws);
  }

  async webSocketClose(ws, code) {
    try { ws.close(code === 1005 || code === 1006 ? 1000 : code, "closing"); } catch (e) {}
  }
}

// A single Durable Object holding the ideas players send from the game: up to 100 characters each, with the
// sender's online name and the game's version. Reading or deleting them needs the dev word, checked against the
// same fingerprint as the game's (DEVW.print in src/game.js; keep the two the same). Anyone can post here
// without the game, so the limits are kept here: IDEA_DAY ideas a day from one address (stored only as a
// fingerprint, and forgotten the next day), IDEAS_DAY from everyone, and the newest IDEAS_KEEP kept.
// Refusals still answer 200 with a reason, so the game's console stays clean.
const DEV_PRINT = "f5fbd99c8a9a458d2267847deb9aab64fd2f699c0746cea014e74f8cc48e6ee5";
const IDEA_LEN = 100, IDEA_DAY = 5, IDEAS_DAY = 300, IDEAS_KEEP = 500;
const sha = async s => [...new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(s)))].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
const reply = o => new Response(JSON.stringify(o), { headers: { "content-type": "application/json", ...CORS } });
const unseen = /[\u0000-\u001f\u007f-\u009f\u200b-\u200f\u2028-\u202e\u2060-\u206f\ufeff]/g;   // control and invisible characters

export class Ideas extends DurableObject {
  async fetch(request) {
    const raw = await request.text();
    if (raw.length > 2000) return reply({ ok: false, why: "long" });
    let b; try { b = JSON.parse(raw); } catch (e) { return reply({ ok: false, why: "bad" }); }
    if (!b || typeof b !== "object") return reply({ ok: false, why: "bad" });
    if (b.op === "send") return this.send(b, request.headers.get("CF-Connecting-IP") || "");
    const print = this.env.DEV_PRINT || DEV_PRINT;   // DEV_PRINT in the environment: a stand-in word for the tests
    if (typeof b.key !== "string" || await sha("retroRack:" + b.key.trim().toLowerCase()) !== print) return reply({ ok: false, why: "key" });
    if (b.op === "list") {
      const all = await this.ctx.storage.list({ prefix: "i:", reverse: true });
      return reply({ ok: true, ideas: [...all].map(([k, v]) => ({ id: k.slice(2), ...v })) });
    }
    if (b.op === "delete" && typeof b.id === "string") { await this.ctx.storage.delete("i:" + b.id.slice(0, 40)); return reply({ ok: true }); }
    if (b.op === "clear") { await this.drop([...(await this.ctx.storage.list({ prefix: "i:" })).keys()]); return reply({ ok: true }); }
    return reply({ ok: false, why: "bad" });
  }

  async send(b, ip) {
    const text = String(b.text || "").replace(/\s/g, " ").replace(unseen, "").replace(/ +/g, " ").trim();   // line breaks and tabs become spaces
    if (!text) return reply({ ok: false, why: "empty" });
    if (text.length > IDEA_LEN) return reply({ ok: false, why: "long" });
    const name = String(b.name || "").replace(unseen, "").replace(/[<>]/g, "").trim().slice(0, 14);
    const v = /^[0-9a-f]{8}$/.test(b.v) ? b.v : "";
    const day = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10), who = (await sha("retroRack-ideas:" + ip)).slice(0, 16);
    let rate = await this.ctx.storage.get("rate");
    if (!rate || rate.day !== day) rate = { day, all: 0, by: {} };
    if ((rate.by[who] || 0) >= IDEA_DAY) return reply({ ok: false, why: "limit", max: IDEA_DAY });
    if (rate.all >= IDEAS_DAY) return reply({ ok: false, why: "busy" });
    rate.by[who] = (rate.by[who] || 0) + 1; rate.all++;
    const t = Date.now();
    await this.ctx.storage.put({ rate, ["i:" + String(t).padStart(15, "0") + "-" + crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 8)]: { t, text, name, v } });
    const all = await this.ctx.storage.list({ prefix: "i:" });
    if (all.size > IDEAS_KEEP) await this.drop([...all.keys()].slice(0, all.size - IDEAS_KEEP));   // the oldest
    return reply({ ok: true, left: IDEA_DAY - rate.by[who] });
  }

  async drop(keys) { for (let i = 0; i < keys.length; i += 128) await this.ctx.storage.delete(keys.slice(i, i + 128)); }   // 128 at a time is the most allowed
}
```

## relay/wrangler.jsonc

Relay settings: Worker name, Durable Object bindings and migrations. (20 lines)

```jsonc
{
  // Cloudflare settings for the Retro Rack relay. The name must match the Worker name in your dashboard.
  "name": "retro-rack-relay",
  "main": "src/index.js",
  "compatibility_date": "2025-06-01",
  "durable_objects": {
    "bindings": [
      { "name": "ROOMS", "class_name": "Room" },
      { "name": "LOBBY", "class_name": "Lobby" },
      { "name": "IDEAS", "class_name": "Ideas" }
    ]
  },
  // free-plan friendly SQLite-backed Durable Objects (v2 adds the open-rooms list, v3 the players' ideas)
  "migrations": [
    { "tag": "v1", "new_sqlite_classes": ["Room"] },
    { "tag": "v2", "new_sqlite_classes": ["Lobby"] },
    { "tag": "v3", "new_sqlite_classes": ["Ideas"] }
  ]
}
```

## sw.js

Service worker for the installable app (network first). (44 lines)

```js
// Retro Rack service worker: lets the installed game start without internet.
// Network first, always: when online, every file comes fresh from the site (so updates arrive straight away and
// both online players get the same version); the saved copy is used only when the network fails.
const CACHE = 'retro-rack';
const FILES = ['./', 'config.js', 'manifest.webmanifest', 'icons/icon-192.png', 'icons/icon-512.png'];

self.addEventListener('install', e => {
  e.waitUntil(caches.open(CACHE)
    .then(c => c.addAll(FILES.map(f => new Request(f, { cache: 'no-cache' }))))
    .then(() => self.skipWaiting()));
});

self.addEventListener('activate', e => {
  e.waitUntil(caches.keys()
    .then(keys => Promise.all(keys.filter(k => k !== CACHE).map(k => caches.delete(k))))
    .then(() => self.clients.claim()));
});

self.addEventListener('fetch', e => {
  const req = e.request, url = new URL(req.url);
  if (req.method !== 'GET' || url.origin !== location.origin) return;
  // the game is one page, so every visit to it shares one saved copy, whatever the ?relay= or #room= part says
  const home = new URL('./', self.registration.scope);
  const page = req.mode === 'navigate' && (url.pathname === home.pathname || url.pathname === home.pathname + 'index.html');
  if (req.mode === 'navigate' && !page) return;   // other pages on the site (README and so on) aren't the game
  const key = page ? home.href : req;
  e.respondWith((async () => {
    try {
      // 'no-cache' checks with the site each time (cheap when nothing changed) instead of trusting the browser cache
      let res = await fetch(page ? req.url : req, { cache: 'no-cache', credentials: 'same-origin' });
      if (page && res.redirected) res = await fetch(req);
      if (res.ok && res.type === 'basic') {
        const copy = res.clone();
        e.waitUntil(caches.open(CACHE).then(c => c.put(key, copy)));
      }
      return res;
    } catch (err) {
      const saved = await caches.match(key, { ignoreSearch: true });
      if (saved) return saved;
      throw err;
    }
  })());
});
```
